This paper proposes a unified framework, KENDO (Kernel ENsemble Disagreement-aware Operator), that integrates Ensemble Gaussian Processes (EGP) with disagreement-aware acquisition strategies. The central idea is to replace hyperparameter sampling with a kernel ensemble and adaptive Bayesian weighting, combined with disagreement-aware acquisition strategies. Within this unified framework, we instantiate KENDO-BO for BO and KENDO-AL for Bayesian AL, demonstrating that both arise from a common self-correcting mechanism with task-specific acquisition objectives. We further extend the approach to multi-objective optimization via random scalarization that preserves the single-optimizer conditioning structure. Thorough numerical tests on synthetic and real-world benchmarks across single-objective optimization, multi-objective optimization, and active learning demonstrate that (i) KENDO-BO achieves competitive or superior optimization performance compared to state-of-the-art methods while reducing computational overhead by up to $5\times$ and (ii) KENDO-AL achieves superior predictive calibration over MCMC-based active learning baselines with up to $27\times$ speedup.

</details>


### [75] [Parameter-Efficient Self-Supervised Adaptation for EEG-FM under Fixed Computational Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24727)
*Meghal Dani,Stefanie Liebe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究证明了仅需更新9%参数的高效自适应方法即可显著提升EEG基础模型在临床任务上的性能，在计算资源和数据收集需求最小化的前提下实现有效部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型通过自监督预训练获得可迁移表示，但在多样化临床数据集上的泛化能力仍然有限。全量微调由于高计算需求在资源受限的临床环境中不切实际，因此需要研究参数高效的自适应方法。

Method: 提出参数高效的自监督适应方法，仅更新9%的参数。在两种具有不同预训练目标的SOTA模型上进行评估：BIOT（对比学习）和CBraMod（掩码重建），并在三个临床EEG数据集上测试：TUAB（异常检测）、TUEV（事件分类）和CHB-MIT（癫痫检测），涵盖分布内和分布外条件。

Result: 自监督适应相比线性探测带来持续的性能提升，AUCPR提升高达20倍。在固定计算预算下，仅需20-50%的可用无标签数据即可达到峰值性能。当总时间窗口数固定时，性能对病人数量不敏感，表明性能仅依赖于整体时间窗口多样性。

Conclusion: 参数高效适应方法使EEG基础模型能够以最小的计算开销和数据收集负担实现有效部署，为资源受限的临床环境提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: EEG foundation models pretrained via self-supervised learning promise transferable representations, but their generalization remains limited, especially across diverse clinical datasets. Full fine-tuning is impractical for resource-constrained clinical settings due to high computational requirements. In this work, we investigate whether parameter-efficient self-supervised adaptation, updating only 9% of parameters suffices to align representations to target tasks. We evaluate our method on two state-of-the-art models with different pretraining objectives: BIOT (contrastive) and CBraMod (masked reconstruction), and evaluate on three clinical EEG datasets for abnormality detection (TUAB), event classification (TUEV), and seizure detection (CHB-MIT) under both in-distribution and out-of-distribution conditions. SSL adaptation yields consistent gains over linear probing, up to 20x AUCPR. Under a fixed compute budget, peak performance requires only 20--50% of available unlabeled data. Critically, when total window count is fixed, performance remains invariant to patient count, suggesting that performance is dependent on overall temporal window diversity only. Our findings demonstrate that parameter-efficient adaptation enables effective deployment of EEG Foundation models (EEG-FM) with minimal computational overhead and data collection burden. Code available at: https://github.com/c3n-group/efficient-eeg-adapt

</details>


### [76] [Optimal Alternating Regret for Online Learning and Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24731)
*Yixin Tao,Weiqiang Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了在线线性优化（OLO）和在线凸优化（OCO）中的极小化最优交替后悔值问题。对于概率单纯形上的OLO，给出了O(log d)的常数交替后悔值算法及匹配下界；对于一般OCO，给出了O(d log(1+T/d))的交替后悔值算法及匹配下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交替后悔值是博弈论中交替学习动力学的核心概念。现有工作的后悔值上界依赖于时间T，且在二人一般和博弈中收敛到粗相关均衡（CCE）时存在额外的log T因子。本文旨在设计具有更紧后悔值界的学习算法，以实现更快的博弈均衡收敛。

Method: 针对概率单纯形上的OLO，设计了具有O(log d)常数交替后悔值的算法；针对d维紧凸集上的一般OCO，设计了具有O(d log(1+T/d))交替后悔值的算法。同时证明了两种情况下的匹配下界。

Result: 1）概率单纯形OLO：O(log d)交替后悔值，与时间T无关，显著改进了之前的O(log^{2/3}d · T^{1/3})结果；2）二人零和博弈：O(log d/T)收敛到纳什均衡；3）二人一般和博弈：O(log d/T)收敛到CCE，首次在解耦学习动力学中实现O(1/T)收敛率，消除了log T因子；4）一般OCO：O(d log(1+T/d))交替后悔值，改进了之前的Õ(d^{2/3}T^{1/3})结果。

Conclusion: 本文为OLO和OCO建立了极小化最优的交替后悔值界，所提出的算法在博弈论应用中实现了更快的均衡收敛。特别是首次在二人一般和博弈中实现了O(1/T)的CCE收敛率，这是解耦学习动力学领域的重要突破。所有上界均配有匹配下界，证明了结果的最优性。

Abstract: We settle the minimax-optimal alternating regret, a regret notion motivated by alternating learning dynamics in games, for both online linear optimization (OLO) and online convex optimization (OCO).
  For OLO over the probability simplex $Δ_d$, we give an algorithm with $O(\log d)$ alternating regret that remains a constant for any time horizon $T$, and a matching lower bound. Our constant regret bound significantly improves previous results with $O(\log ^{2/3}d \cdot T^{1/3})$ regret [Cevher, Cutkosky, Kavis, Piliouras, Skoulakis, Viano, NeurIPS 2023, Hait, Li, Luo, Zhang, COLT 2025]. As a result, we obtain alternating learning dynamics with $O(\log d /T)$ convergence to Nash equilibria in two-player zero-sum games and $O(\log d /T)$ convergence to coarse correlated equilibria in two-player general-sum games. This is the first uncoupled learning dynamics with $O(1/T)$ convergence to CCE in two-player general-sum games, while all prior works suffer additional $\log T$ factors.
  For general OCO over a $d$-dimensional compact convex set, we give an algorithm with $O(d\log (1+T/d))$ alternating regret, improving the previous best of $\widetilde{O}(d^{2/3}T^{1/3})$. We also prove a matching lower bound of $Ω(d\log (1+T/d))$, showing that the $Ω(\log T)$ factor is unavoidable.

</details>


### [77] [$(\text{DNN})^2$: Doubly Non-Negative Relaxations for Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24743)
*Hanna Jiamei Zhang,Alan Papalia,Michael Everett,David M. Rosen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了(DNN)²方法，一种针对ReLU神经网络验证的新型算法，通过解决双重非负规划(DNN)中的最优性证书非唯一性问题，实现了比标准SDP方法更紧的边界和可扩展的验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的线性规划(LP)和半定规划(SDP)松弛方法在ReLU神经网络验证中产生过于保守的安全保证，存在显著的松弛间隙。虽然完全正规划(CPP)可以闭合这个间隙，但它是NP难问题。其最便宜的可行松弛——双重非负规划(DNN)比标准SDP更紧，但由于额外非负性约束导致对偶乘子非唯一，无法使用标准认证方法。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的特征值最大化过程，在非唯一乘子空间中搜索有效的证书（即全局最优性保证）。该方法基于Burer-Monteiro(BM)分解实现可扩展性，同时解决了DNN公式中非负性约束导致的最优性认证问题。

Result: 实验表明(DNN)²方法产生的边界持续比标准SDP方法更紧，通常能够匹配精确解；当存在有效证书时，认证过程能够确认全局最优性。

Conclusion: 这些结果是为安全关键自主系统中神经网络控制器和感知模块部署所需的紧致、可认证、计算可扩展的验证保证迈出的关键一步。

Abstract: Existing linear program (LP) and semidefinite program (SDP) relaxations for rectified linear unit (ReLU) neural network (NN) verification yield overly-conservative safety guarantees due to significant relaxation gaps. While the completely positive program (CPP) formulation closes this gap, it is NP-hard to solve. Its cheapest tractable relaxation, the doubly non-negative program (DNN), retains critical constraints as an SDP, but one whose size exceeds the reach of interior-point methods at practical scale. While Burer-Monteiro (BM) factorization has been applied to make SDP-based verification scalable, no such result exists for the strictly tighter DNN formulation. A key obstacle is that additional non-negativity constraints in the DNN cause dual multipliers for optimality certification to be non-unique, making standard certification methods inapplicable. We propose a novel eigenvalue maximization procedure that searches the non-unique multiplier space for a valid certificate, i.e. a global optimality guarantee. Experiments demonstrate that our approach $(\text{DNN})^2$ produces bounds consistently tighter than the standard SDP method, often matching the exact solution, and that our certification procedure confirms global optimality when a valid certificate exists. These results are a key step toward providing tight, certifiable, and computationally scalable verification guarantees needed to deploy neural network controllers and perception modules in safety-critical autonomous systems.

</details>


### [78] [Beyond Uniform Local Isometry and Topology: FactoMap for Disentangled Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24762)
*Sohini Gupta,Bahareh Tolooshams*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出因子空间结构框架和FactoMap方法，通过引入因子空间结构（包括因子域、生成器诱导的识别和位置相关的尺度）来解决传统解耦方法使用欧几里得乘积坐标表示生成因子时的几何不匹配问题，实现了对拓扑等价但几何不同空间的区分和有效解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的解耦方法使用欧几里得乘积坐标表示生成因子，但底层因子空间可能存在卷曲、塌缩或具有位置相关的几何特性。统计独立的因子在几何上可能不可分离，例如色调和尺度会产生以不同速率增长的效应，导致无法通过固定重缩放消除的各向异性。这种几何结构的不匹配促使需要新的方法来捕获因子空间的实际拓扑和几何特性。

Method: 提出因子空间结构框架，结合因子域、生成器诱导的识别和位置相关尺度来区分拓扑等价但因子几何不同的空间。在此基础上提出因子空间地形图（FactoMap），该方法学习由因子空间格子索引的可解释原型，通过地形学习将格子的周期性、塌缩和非均匀范围转移到表示中，使表示结构与底层因子空间的几何特性相匹配。

Result: 实验结果表明，匹配因子空间结构能够保持因子连续性并实现对底层因子的有效解耦。FactoMap成功地将因子空间的周期性、塌缩和非均匀范围等几何特性转移到学习到的表示中，解决了传统方法因几何不匹配导致的解耦困难。

Conclusion: 因子空间的几何结构对于解耦表示学习至关重要。通过显式建模因子空间的结构特性（包括拓扑等价空间的几何差异、位置相关的尺度变化等），可以显著改善解耦性能。FactoMap提供了一种有效的方法来捕获和利用这些结构特性，为解耦表示学习提供了新的理论框架和实践方法。

Abstract: Many disentanglement methods represent generative factors using Euclidean product coordinates, although the underlying factor spaces may wrap, collapse, or have position-dependent geometry. We introduce factor-space structure, combining factor domains, generator-induced identifications, and position-dependent scales to distinguish topologically equivalent spaces with different factor geometries. We show that statistically independent factors need not be geometrically separable: hue and scale produce effects that grow at different rates, yielding anisotropy that no fixed rescaling removes. We propose the Factor-Space Topographic Map (FactoMap), which learns interpretable prototypes indexed by a factor-space lattice. Topographic learning transfers the lattice's periodicity, collapses, and non-uniform extent to the representation. Experiments show that matching this structure preserves factor continuity and enables disentanglement of the underlying factors.

</details>


### [79] [LION: A Clifford Neural Paradigm for Multimodal-Attributed Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24795)
*Xunkai Li,Zekai Chen,Zhengyu Wu,Henan Sun,Daohan Su,Guang Zeng,Hongchao Qin,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于Clifford代数的LION神经网络范式，通过几何流形实现模态对齐，通过自适应全息聚合实现模态融合，在多模态属性图任务上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在多模态属性图上存在两个关键局限：（1）模态对齐时忽略图上下文，使用拓扑约束或模态特定的算子作为分词器，抑制了模态交互；（2）模态融合时缺乏适应性，简单适配双模态图，未能充分利用带有拓扑先验的对齐token，导致泛化性差和性能退化。

Method: 基于Clifford代数和解耦图神经网络范式（传播后聚合），实现先对齐后融合的框架。首先构建基于Clifford代数的模态感知几何流形，通过几何诱导的高阶图传播实现模态交互和对齐；然后基于对齐token的拓扑感知Clifford分量，提出自适应全息聚合模块，集成分量级能量和传播尺度信息，通过可学习参数优化模态融合。

Result: 在9个文本-图像多模态属性图数据集上进行了广泛实验，LION在3个图下游任务和3个模态下游任务上均显著优于当前最优基线方法。

Conclusion: 基于Clifford代数的几何方法能有效建模多模态属性图中的复杂关系，通过解耦的对齐-融合范式实现了模态交互与拓扑信息的深度整合，为多模态图学习提供了新的理论基础和实用框架。

Abstract: Recently, the rapid advancement of multimodal domains has driven a data-centric paradigm shift in graph ML, transitioning from text-attributed to multimodal-attributed graphs. This advancement significantly enhances data representation and expands the scope of graph downstream tasks, such as modality-oriented tasks, thereby improving the practical utility of graph ML. Despite its promise, limitations exist in the current neural paradigms:(1) Neglect Context in Modality Alignment: Most existing methods adopt topology-constrained or modality-specific operators as tokenizers.These aligners inevitably neglect graph context and inhibit modality interaction, resulting in suboptimal alignment.(2) Lack of Adaptation in Modality Fusion: Most existing methods are simple adaptations for 2-modality graphs and fail to adequately exploit aligned tokens equipped with topology priors during fusion, leading to poor generalizability and performance degradation.To address the above issues, we propose LION (c\underline{LI}ff\underline{O}rd \underline{N}eural paradigm) based on the Clifford algebra and decoupled graph neural paradigm (i.e., propagation-then-aggregation) to implement alignment-then-fusion in multimodal-attributed graphs. Specifically, we first construct a modality-aware geometric manifold grounded in Clifford algebra.This geometric-induced high-order graph propagation efficiently achieves modality interaction, facilitating modality alignment.Then, based on the topology-aware Clifford components of aligned tokens, we propose adaptive holographic aggregation. This module integrates component-wise energy and propagation-scale information with learnable parameters to improve modality fusion. Extensive experiments on 9 text-image MAG datasets demonstrate that LION significantly outperforms SOTA baselines across 3 graph and 3 modality downstream tasks.

</details>


### [80] [MDTE: Minority-Aware Diffusion over Temporal Edge Events for Imbalanced Node Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24812)
*Zhou Zelong,Zhang Tianming,Yang Zhengyi,Tang Yifu,Hou Chenyu,Cao Bin,Fan Jing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MDTE框架，通过条件扩散去噪和分布感知的选择性传播，有效解决了时序图节点分类中的类别不平衡问题，在少数类指标上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序图上的类别不平衡节点分类面临两大挑战：（1）多数类主导的时序传播会逐渐同化少数类的表示；（2）传统的节点和邻居信息为少数类提供的判别性证据不足。

Method: 提出MDTE框架，包含两个核心模块：（1）分布感知选择性传播（Distribution-Aware Selective Propagation），结合基于LOF的传播过滤和聚类感知的低频传播，在保留有信息邻居依赖的同时缓解有害传播；（2）多视图判别融合（Multi-View Discriminative Fusion），利用特征重构和拓扑预测来刻画类别分布差异，提取互补的判别性信号指导去噪过程。

Result: 在五个真实数据集上的实验表明，MDTE在少数类指标上持续取得最佳性能：相比最强基线，少数类召回率提升高达23.53个百分点，少数类F1提升8.68个百分点，AUPRC提升2.67个百分点。

Conclusion: MDTE通过条件扩散去噪重构稳定且具有判别性的时序边事件表示，有效缓解了多数类信息同化问题，为时序图上的类别不平衡问题提供了一个有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Class-imbalanced node classification on temporal graphs is challenging because majority-dominated temporal propagation progressively assimilates minority representations, while conventional node and neighborhood information provides insufficient discriminative evidence for minority classes. To address these issues, we propose MDTE, a minority-aware diffusion framework that reconstructs stable and discriminative temporal edge-event representations through conditional diffusion denoising. Specifically, MDTE introduces Distribution-Aware Selective Propagation, which combines Local Outlier Factor (LOF)-based propagation filtering with cluster-aware low-frequency propagation. The module preserves informative neighborhood dependencies while mitigating harmful propagation and majority-class information assimilation. It further develops Multi-View Discriminative Fusion, which exploits feature reconstruction and topology prediction to characterize class-wise differences in distribution learning and extracts complementary discriminability signals to guide denoising. Experiments on five real-world datasets demonstrate that MDTE consistently achieves the best performance on minority-class-oriented metrics, improving minority-class recall by up to 23.53 percentage points, minority-class F1 by 8.68 percentage points, and AUPRC by 2.67 percentage points over the strongest baselines.

</details>


### [81] [Effective Learning Rate Governs Loss Dynamics in Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24814)
*Zihan Liu,Ruiheng Zheng,Shaobo Zhang,Changxin Tian,Kunlong Chen,Zhiqiang Zhang,Lei Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 发现语言模型预训练中存在有效学习率（ELR）坍缩现象：学习率与参数范数的比值（ELR）主导损失动态，当ELR匹配时，损失轨迹坍缩，揭示了连接LR调度、范数控制和损失动态的共同坐标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索语言模型预训练中学习率和参数范数如何影响训练动态，以及不同配置下损失曲线的内在联系，寻找统一的理论框架来解释损失动态的规律性。

Method: 通过跨优化器、架构、数据集和模型尺度的广泛实验验证ELR坍缩现象；系统消融实验识别坍缩精度的关键决定因素；控制干预分析权重衰减和Hyperball的影响；建立基于ELR的函数缩放定律（FSL）。

Result: ELR匹配的不同训练运行的损失轨迹坍缩，平均坍缩误差约10^-3量级，低于种子间变异；归一化设计和LR-范数变化时间尺度是坍缩精度的关键决定因素；权重衰减和Hyperball主要通过诱导ELR调度来影响损失动态；基于ELR的FSL可跨范数控制方法迁移并解释延迟加速效应。

Conclusion: 有效学习率（ELR）是连接学习率调度、范数控制和损失动态的共同坐标，为理解和优化语言模型预训练提供了统一的理论框架，具有广泛的适用性和实践指导意义。

Abstract: We uncover ELR collapse in language model pretraining: learning rate (LR) and parameter norm govern loss dynamics primarily through their ratio, the effective learning rate (ELR). When ELR is matched across runs, their loss trajectories collapse throughout training despite substantially different LRs and parameter norms. Across optimizers, architectures, datasets, and model scales, mean collapse errors are typically a few x 10^-3, below the seed-to-seed variation measured in a representative configuration. Systematic ablations identify normalization design and the timescale of LR-norm variation as key determinants of collapse precision. Controlled interventions further show that weight decay and Hyperball shape loss dynamics primarily through the ELR schedules they induce. Replacing LR with ELR enables a fitted functional scaling law (FSL) to transfer across norm-control methods. The resulting ELR-based FSL also explains delayed acceleration, a recurring effect of norm control. Together, these results establish ELR as a common coordinate linking LR scheduling, norm control, and loss dynamics.

</details>


### [82] [A Geometric Theory of Robust Fairness Audits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24818)
*Binita Maity*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了邻域公平性审计方法的鲁棒性问题，提出了一个几何框架来分析有界扰动下审计结果的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于邻域的公平性审计方法依赖最近邻关系来评估个体公平性，但缺少对这些审计程序本身鲁棒性的研究。由于小扰动会改变局部邻域从而导致公平性评估结果改变，即使模型预测本身未变，因此需要分析审计程序的稳定性。

Method: 作者开发了一个几何框架来分析有界扰动下邻域公平性审计的鲁棒性：建立了邻域不变性的充分条件，量化邻域替换如何传播导致审计不稳定性，并引入了审计波动性这一新度量指标来衡量重复扰动下公平性审计的预期敏感性。

Result: 理论分析得到了邻域不变性的充分条件以及邻域替换传播的量化分析。在基准数据集上的实验支持了理论分析，表明所提出的框架能够解释观察到的邻域公平性审计的稳定性。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统地研究了邻域公平性审计的鲁棒性问题，提出的几何框架为理解和预测审计结果的稳定性提供了理论基础，有助于提高公平性审计的可靠性。

Abstract: Neighborhood-based fairness audits evaluate individual fairness by comparing predictions among similar individuals in feature space. Despite their widespread use, little is known about the robustness of the auditing procedure itself. Because these audits rely on nearest neighbor relationships, small perturbations in feature space can alter local neighborhoods and produce different fairness assessments even when model predictions remain unchanged. We develop a geometric framework for analyzing the robustness of neighborhood-based fairness audits under bounded perturbations. Our analysis establishes sufficient conditions for neighborhood invariance, quantifies how neighborhood replacement propagates to audit instability, and introduces audit volatility, a measure of the expected sensitivity of fairness audits under repeated perturbations. Experiments on benchmark datasets support the theoretical analysis and show that the proposed framework explains the observed stability of neighborhood-based fairness audits.

</details>


### [83] [BioKERN: Biological Kernel Regularization for Histology-to-Transcriptomics Neighborhood Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24823)
*Seungik Cho,Betul Orcan-Ekmekci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BioKERN是一个多模态空间表示学习框架，通过将生物结构作为可学习的归纳偏置，结合转录组相似性和空间邻近性构建生物核，在Mouse Brain Visium和Human Liver数据集上显著提升了生物邻域检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的组织学-转录组学学习方法过于强调实例级别的精确匹配，忽略了非配对样本之间共享的分子或空间上下文信息。空间生物学需要保留生物邻域结构的表示，而不仅仅是跨模态的精确对应关系。

Method: BioKERN构建了一个训练时的生物核(biological kernel)，通过结合转录组相似性和空间邻近性来提供分级邻域监督，并正则化嵌入几何结构。该框架采用固定的、模型无关的生物邻域定义进行评估，将生物结构作为显式的、可学习的归纳偏置。

Result: 在Mouse Brain Visium和Human Liver GSE240429数据集上，BioKERN在单尺度和多尺度设置下的生物邻域检索性能均优于BLEEP方法。通过控制共享架构的实验证明，性能提升主要来源于生物核正则化，而非模型容量的增加。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，显式的生物几何结构可以作为空间生物学多模态学习的可解释归纳偏置，为该领域提供了一个有效的学习范式。

Abstract: Spatially resolved biology requires representations that preserve biological neighborhood structure rather than only exact cross-modal correspondences. Existing histology--transcriptomics objectives can emphasize instance-level matching even when non-paired spots share molecular or spatial context. We introduce BioKERN, a multimodal spatial representation-learning framework that incorporates biological structure as an explicit, learnable inductive bias. BioKERN constructs a training-time biological kernel by combining transcriptomic similarity and spatial proximity, then uses it to provide graded neighborhood supervision and regularize embedding geometry. Evaluation uses a fixed, model-independent biological neighborhood definition shared by all methods. Across Mouse Brain Visium and Human Liver GSE240429, BioKERN consistently improves biological-neighborhood retrieval over BLEEP in both single- and multi-scale settings. Controlled shared-architecture experiments show that most of the improvement arises from biological-kernel regularization rather than increased model capacity. These results support explicit biological geometry as an interpretable inductive bias for multimodal learning in spatial biology.

</details>


### [84] [Bellman Calibration for Marginalized Importance Weighting in Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24858)
*Lars van der Laan,Nathan Kallus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种模型无关的后处理方法——等渗贝尔曼校准，用于减少离线策略评估中的占用平衡违规问题，并提供理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的边际重要性加权估计方法（如极小极大、原始对偶和拟合定点估计器）由于函数类近似、正则化或不完全优化会产生占用平衡违规。这些违规难以诊断和减少，因为目标函数通常缺乏直接的监督验证损失来进行超参数调优、模型选择和早停。

Method: 提出等渗贝尔曼校准，这是一种一维的、模型无关的后处理方法。该方法通过在一维非递减变换类上应用拟合占用比评估（FORE），校正初始占用比估计的尺度和形状，同时保留排序信息。该方法被刻画为条件定点性质，等价于对校准比的每个测试函数满足占用平衡。

Result: 推导了校准-精化边界，表明任何具有小校准误差的拟合比在其拟合值基础上几乎与最佳后处理一样好。为等渗贝尔曼校准建立了有限样本校准保证和相对于初始估计的最佳单调变换的KL oracle不等式。该方法在校准误差和KL风险方面达到了与最佳单调校正相当的性能。

Conclusion: 等渗贝尔曼校准成功减少了占用平衡违规，并为目标占用泛函（包括策略价值估计）提供了下游保证，在统计误差范围内实现了接近最佳单调校正的性能。

Abstract: Marginalized importance weighting evaluates a target policy by reweighting offline state-action samples with its discounted occupancy ratio, characterized by an adjoint Bellman equation. Existing minimax, primal-dual, and fitted fixed-point estimators can leave residual occupancy-balance violations because of function-class approximation, regularization, or incomplete optimization. These violations are difficult to diagnose and reduce because the objectives generally lack a direct supervised validation loss for hyperparameter tuning, model selection, and early stopping. We introduce isotonic Bellman calibration, a one-dimensional, model-agnostic post-processing method that reduces these violations while preserving the ranking information in any initial occupancy-ratio estimate. The method corrects the estimate's scale and shape by applying fitted occupancy-ratio evaluation (FORE) over a one-dimensional class of nondecreasing transformations. We characterize Bellman calibration as a conditional fixed-point property equivalent to occupancy-balance against every test function of the calibrated ratio. More generally, we derive a calibration-refinement bound showing that any fitted ratio with small calibration error performs nearly as well as the best post-processing based on its fitted values. For isotonic Bellman calibration, we establish finite-sample calibration guarantees and a KL oracle inequality relative to the best monotone transformation of the initial estimate. Consequently, isotonic Bellman calibration achieves small calibration error and KL risk within statistical error of the best monotone correction, with guarantees for downstream target-occupancy functionals, including policy-value estimation.

</details>


### [85] [Improving Cross-Problem Vehicle Routing with Locally Augmented Preferences and Representation Disentanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24859)
*Arthur Corrêa,Paulo Nascimento,Samuel Moniz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了POLAR训练算法和PLE编码器架构，显著提升了多任务车辆路径问题(VRP)求解器的性能，在分布内变体上相对最强基线缩小了21.3%的平均差距，在32个未见变体中的27个上超越了先前的神经方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多任务VRP求解器存在两个主要局限：在训练方面，强化学习面临奖励尺度差异和优势信号缩减问题，偏好优化在采样路径趋于相同时会停滞，导致训练后期监督信号微弱；在架构方面，现有的全共享编码器会将不同变体的约束相关表示纠缠在一起，限制了泛化能力。

Method: 本文提出了两个模型无关的贡献：1) POLAR算法：在形成偏好对之前，对最佳解码路径应用局部搜索细化，从而产生更有信息量的成对边界；2) PLE编码器：通过门控机制将每个编码器层路由到一个共享专家和一组任务特定专家，逐步将公共路由结构与约束特定编码分离。

Result: 在16个分布内变体上，相对最强发布基线平均差距缩小21.3%；在32个未见变体中的27个上优于先前的神经方法。消融研究证实每个贡献的有效性，表明两者都能跨多种骨干模型架构改善跨问题泛化能力。

Conclusion: POLAR训练算法和PLE编码器架构有效解决了多任务VRP求解器在训练监督和架构表示方面的核心问题，显著提升了当前神经多任务求解器的最先进水平，证明了所提方法在分布内和分布外泛化方面的优越性。

Abstract: Multi-task vehicle routing problem (VRP) solvers seek to handle multiple VRP variants within a single unified model, avoiding the need to train a separate model for every variant. In spite of recent progress, current approaches remain limited on two fronts. On the training side, reinforcement learning suffers from reward-scale disparities and shrinking advantage signals as policies improve, whereas preference optimization stagnates once sampled tours become near-identical and thus fundamentally limited by the quality of the policy's own generated solutions, leaving both paradigms with weak supervision as training progresses. On the architecture side, existing fully shared encoders entangle constraint-dependent representations across heterogeneous variants, which limits generalization. We address these gaps with two model-agnostic contributions. First, we propose Preference Optimization with Locally Augmented Refinement (POLAR), a novel training algorithm that applies a local search refinement pass to the best decoded tour before forming preference pairs, yielding much more informative pairwise margins. Second, a Progressive Layered Extraction (PLE) encoder routes each encoder layer through one shared expert and a set of task-specific experts via a gating mechanism, progressively separating common routing structure from constraint-specific encodings. Through extensive experiments on various VRP variants, we show that POLAR and PLE together elevate the current state-of-the-art among neural multi-task solvers. We reduce the average gap to reference solutions by 21.3% relative to the strongest published baseline on 16 in-distribution variants, and outperform prior neural methods on 27 out of 32 unseen variants. Ablation studies confirm the efficacy of each contribution, showing that both improve cross-problem generalization across multiple backbone model architectures.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [86] [Place, Slice and Schedule: Hierarchical O-RAN Control of a Tethered mmWave UAV-gNB](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23824)
*Alireza Mohammadhosseini,Fatemeh Afghah*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于O-RAN架构的分层控制方案，通过非实时和近实时RIC协同优化无人机基站的部署位置与切片资源配置，显著提升了eMBB和URLLC业务的服务质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机载5G NR基站的灵活部署特性使其物理拓扑与无线资源管理产生耦合关系：无人机的移动会改变阻塞情况、信道质量和用户服务集合，而业务需求、队列状态和服务要求的变化速度更快。现有的O-RAN无人机研究通常独立优化轨迹、部署、关联或资源分配，缺乏对慢速空中控制与快速用户调度的协调，无法有效应对这种多时间尺度的耦合挑战。

Method: 采用O-RAN解耦架构、KPI监控和多时间尺度RIC控制实现分层优化：非实时RIC rApp利用聚合KPI和无线环境上下文联合控制系留无人机位置和eMBB/URLLC切片预算；近实时RIC xApp在该预算约束下进行用户级资源分配。xApp实现为置换等变的DeepSets Soft Actor-Critic (D-SAC)调度器，将用户视为无序集合处理，在Sionna RT射线追踪信道环境中进行训练。

Result: 分层控制器相比传统和学习调度器将eMBB SLA满足率提升最高17%，URLLC按时交付率提升最高42%；学习型rApp相比基线方法将URLLC按时交付率进一步提升最高20%。

Conclusion: 通过O-RAN架构实现的多时间尺度分层控制能够有效协调无人机基站的慢速移动控制与快速用户调度，为无人机辅助的5G网络提供了可行的跨层优化解决方案，显著改善了关键业务性能指标。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV)-mounted 5G New Radio base stations (gNBs) can augment terrestrial networks with an on-demand, repositionable Frequency Range 2 (FR2) capacity layer. This flexibility, however, couples the physical network topology with radio-resource management: UAV movement reshapes blockage, channel quality, and the set of effectively served users, while traffic demand, queues, and service requirements evolve at a much faster timescale. Existing Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN)-enabled UAV studies optimize trajectory, deployment, association, or resource allocation, but typically in isolation, without coordinating slow aerial control with fast per-user scheduling. We instead exploit O-RAN disaggregation, Key Performance Indicator (KPI) monitoring, and multi-timescale RAN Intelligent Controller (RIC) control to address this coupling: a Non-Real-Time RIC rApp uses aggregated KPIs and radio-environment context to jointly control tethered UAV placement and the enhanced Mobile Broadband (eMBB)/Ultra-Reliable Low-Latency Communication (URLLC) slice budget, while a Near-Real-Time RIC xApp allocates per-user resources within that budget. We realize this xApp as a permutation-equivariant DeepSets Soft Actor-Critic (D-SAC) scheduler that treats the users as an unordered set, trained in a Sionna RT ray traced channel. The resulting hierarchical controller improves eMBB SLA satisfaction by up to 17% and URLLC on-time delivery by up to 42% over classical and learned schedulers; the learned rApp further raises URLLC on-time delivery by up to 20% over baselines.

</details>


### [87] [BotScan: An adaptive active probing approach for identifying live IoT Botnet C2 servers at scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23854)
*S M Maksudul Alam,Vivek Jain,Zhaowei Tan,Srikanth V. Krishnamurthy,Michalis Faloutsos*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: BotScan是一种大规模主动探测方法，通过协议重放和空间局部性利用，实现了IoT僵尸网络C2服务器的高效发现，相比基线方法探测效率提升约一倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有僵尸网络C2服务器的大规模主动发现面临资源效率挑战，需要在计算开销和探测数据包数量方面实现优化，以支持对大型IP空间的高效扫描。

Method: ①协议重放方法：分析IoT恶意软件二进制文件，测量和分类C2通信协议的"可重放性"，识别可被重放的协议模式；②行为自适应探测策略：采用两级分段中心方法利用C2服务器的空间局部性，并根据探测结果动态调整探测策略。

Result: 在1,842个IoT恶意软件样本和250万IP地址空间上的验证表明：①至少72%的恶意软件样本支持协议重放；②在相同探测数量下发现的活跃C2服务器数量约为基线方法的两倍；③识别出896个活跃服务器，包括112个未报告的C2服务器。

Conclusion: BotScan证明了利用IoT僵尸网络协议简化和C2服务器行为模式（空间局部性）可以有效提升大规模主动探测的效率，为自动化C2服务器发现提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: How can we actively search and identify live C2 servers of botnets at scale? The scalability requirement introduces the need to utilize resources efficiently in terms of computation and number of probing packets. We propose BotScan, an approach for actively probing a large IP space to find the highest possible number of live C2 servers. The novelty of BotScan revolves around two insights, which we establish empirically. First, contrary to popular PC-centric observations, many modern IoT botnet communication protocols use packets with minimal customization, which we observe across six major families. Second, C2 servers exhibit exploitable behavioral patterns, such as strong spatial locality. We substantiate the first insight by developing a streamlined approach where, given malware binaries, we measure and taxonomize the "replayability" of its C2 communication protocol. Then, we introduce a behavior-adaptive probing strategy that: (a) exploits the spatial locality of C2 servers using a two-level segment-centric approach, and (b) adapts dynamically to the success of its probes. We validate the effectiveness of our method using 1,842 recently collected IoT binaries, and we explore a target space of 2.5M IP addresses. First, a replay-based method is applicable for at least 72% of the malware binaries. Second, our method outperforms baseline methods by finding approximately double the live C2 servers for the same number of probes. We also conduct two case-studies where we identify 896 live servers including 112 unreported C2 servers.

</details>


### [88] [Ray-Traced Augmentation for Signal Strength Based Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23901)
*Jihoon Og,Ningze Sun,Ioanis Nikolaidis,Omid Ardakanian*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于光线追踪的框架，通过生成合成RSS指纹来大幅减少室内定位对真实指纹收集的依赖，仅使用合成数据训练即可达到3.05米的定位误差，比最佳基线方法提升33.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于Wi-Fi的室内定位需要大量收集真实的接收信号强度(RSS)指纹，导致部署成本高且耗时，迫切需要减少对真实指纹收集的依赖。

Method: 提出基于光线追踪的框架：(1)通过贝叶斯优化使用少量真实RSS指纹校准建筑模型；(2)进行逐接入点校准以消除模拟RSS值的残差；(3)使用校准后的模型生成大规模合成RSS指纹数据集；(4)引入新颖的二进制和多值RSS表示方法；(5)设计支持2.4GHz和5GHz测量跨频段融合的ResNet定位架构。

Result: 在真实校园建筑测试中，仅使用合成数据训练时，采用多值表示和上游跨频段融合的定位方法在真实数据测试集上达到3.05米平均定位误差，比最佳基线方法提升33.6%，证明了校准的光线追踪模拟能够有效替代真实指纹收集。

Conclusion: 校准的基于光线追踪的仿真可以大幅减少对真实RSS指纹的需求，同时实现准确的基于深度学习的室内定位，为降低室内定位系统部署成本提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Indoor localization based on Wi-Fi typically relies on extensive collection of real-world received signal strength (RSS) fingerprints, making deployment costly and time-consuming. We present a ray-tracing-based framework that reduces this reliance by generating synthetic RSS fingerprints from a building model. We first calibrate the building model using a small amount of real RSS fingerprints through Bayesian optimization, followed by per-access-point calibration to account for residual errors in simulated RSS values. The calibrated model is then used to generate a large augmented dataset of synthetic RSS fingerprints at arbitrary locations. To effectively exploit these data for localization, we introduce novel binary and multivalued representations of RSS values and a ResNet-based localization architecture that supports cross-band fusion of 2.4 and 5 GHz measurements. We evaluate our localization method on a real campus building against a diverse set of four baselines. When trained exclusively on synthetic data, the proposed method with multivalued representation and upstream cross-band fusion achieves a mean localization error of 3.05m on a real-data test set, outperforming the best baseline by 33.6%. The results demonstrate that calibrated ray-tracing-based simulation can substantially reduce the need for real RSS fingerprints while enabling accurate deep-learning-based indoor localization.

</details>


### [89] [Centrality-Based Deployment of Queue Policies in Acyclic Multipath Routing Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24131)
*Mahima Gupta,Acquin Biju,Rijul Jain,Dipesh Sharma,Sreelakshmi Manjunath*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究具有拓扑层次结构、无环路径和多路径路由的网络中的AQM部署问题，提出基于Katz中心性的路由器选择指标，通过稳定性分析和仿真验证了该策略能有效降低排队延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过度的排队延迟对延迟敏感的网络应用构成重大阻碍。尽管主动队列管理(AQM)策略被认为是必要的解决方案，但实际部署仍然稀少，因此需要研究在特定网络结构中如何有效部署AQM。

Method: 使用TCP和队列动态的流体模型以及简单的基于阈值的队列策略建立网络闭环模型。通过稳定性分析揭示网络稳定性与RTT的关系，并定义基于Katz中心性的度量指标来选择最适合部署AQM的路由器。

Result: 稳定性分析表明：随着TCP流平均RTT增加，网络容易出现不稳定；但在合适的路由器上部署基于阈值的AQM后，网络能够容忍更大的RTT而不失稳定性。基于Katz中心性的部署策略能确保最大的稳定效果，数据包级仿真证实该策略确保了网络的低延迟运行。

Conclusion: 在具有拓扑层次结构的网络中，通过基于Katz中心性的指标选择合适的路由器部署AQM，可以显著提升网络稳定性，容忍更大的RTT变化，并确保低延迟的网络运行，为AQM的实际部署提供了理论指导和实践策略。

Abstract: Excessive queueing delays constitute a significant impediment to latency-sensitive network applications. Although effective deployment of Active Queue Management (AQM) strategies has been proposed as a necessary solution, deployment remains sparse. This paper studies AQM deployment in a specific class of networks where routers/switches have a topological hierarchy, form acyclic paths, and adopt multipath routing. Our approach rests on the well-established premise that AQM deployment impacts packet-forwarding dynamics in networks carrying TCP flows, thus establishing a direct link between stability and network performance. We use fluid models for TCP and queue dynamics in the network, along with a simple threshold-based queue policy to outline a closed-loop model for the network. Stability analyses reveal that while the network is vulnerable to instability as the average round-trip time (RTT) of the TCP flows increases, it tolerates a much larger RTT without losing stability when the threshold-based AQM is deployed in an appropriate router. We then define a Katz centrality-based metric to choose the most appropriate router for AQM deployment, and argue that doing so ensures the greatest stabilising effect. Finally, packet-level simulations corroborate that the proposed deployment strategy ensures low-latency operation of the network.

</details>


### [90] [WiCi: Wireless GPU Computing Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24204)
*Yibin Shen,Wei Li,Kaiqiang Xu,Zili Meng*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了WiCi（无线GPU计算基础设施），使移动设备能够通过WiFi无线访问服务器级GPU，将推理任务卸载到附近GPU上执行，显著提升了移动端的LLM推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理需求呈指数级增长，GPU推理使用量已超过训练。边缘推理因移动性限制性能不佳，而云端推理成本高昂且不可持续，特别是在智能代理范式下成本持续增加。因此需要让移动设备获得服务器级GPU的强大计算能力。

Method: 提出无线GPU计算基础设施（WiCi），移动设备通过WiFi无线访问服务器级GPU，在移动客户端运行推理任务，但将GPU相关计算卸载到附近的GPU上。WiCi包含一系列设计确保基础设施具有应用可扩展性、设备兼容性，以及与物理GPU相当的性能。

Result: 与移动设备本地推理相比，WiCi可将首个Token时间减少高达90%，Token生成速率提升约39倍，并支持更大的模型。在不同应用中，WiCi可达到服务器级GPU原生性能的近80%。

Conclusion: WiCi成功实现了移动设备对服务器级GPU的无线访问，为移动端LLM推理提供了一种高效、低成本的解决方案，在性能和成本之间取得了良好平衡，为推理服务提供商提供了可行的云端替代方案。

Abstract: LLM inference applications are gaining significant traction. The demand for inference is growing exponentially, and the GPU usage of inference is increasingly surpassing that of training. Due to the mobility penalty, edge-side inference fails to deliver satisfactory performance. Consequently, most inference service providers currently rely on cloud-based inference, which incurs substantial, not sustainable costs for enterprises, and is even increasing in the agentic paradigm. Therefore, our goal is to enable powerful computing capabilities as server-grade GPUs on mobile devices. We propose Wireless GPU Computing Infrastructure (WiCi) in this paper. Through WiCi, mobile devices can wirelessly access server-grade GPUs, running inference tasks on mobile clients but offloading GPU-related computations to a nearby GPU via WiFi. WiCi introduces a series of designs to make sure the infrastructure is scalable with different applications, compatible with different mobile devices, and has comparable performance to running on a physical GPU. We test WiCi from mobile devices and find that WiCi can reduce time to first token by up to 90%, improve the token rate by approximately 39x compared to local inference on mobile devices for the same model, and support much larger models. WiCi also achieves up to nearly 80% of the native performance of the server-grade GPU across different applications.

</details>


### [91] [LEMONS: Leveraging Model-Based Techniques to Enable Non-Intrusive Semantic Enrichment in Wireless Sensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24277)
*Jan Novacek,Arthur Kühlwein,Sebastian Reiter,Alexander Viehl,Oliver Bringmann,Wolfgang Rosenstiel*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合模型驱动软件开发与语义网技术的方法，实现无线传感器网络中传感器数据的高效语义增强，从而提升数据互操作性并简化网络配置与管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线传感器网络的设置与维护通常复杂且耗时，缺乏数据互操作性限制了数据共享与重用，而现有的大规模WSN管理方法需要专家知识，门槛较高。

Method: 将模型驱动软件开发（MDSD）与语义网技术（SWT）相结合，设计通用的模型化、类型无关的配置方案；提出一种通用架构并实现原型系统，通过语义标注对传感器数据进行非侵入式增强。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法能够提供非侵入式的语义增强，计算开销在亚毫秒级别，并实现了WSN的局部自动化配置，同时降低了对专家知识的依赖。

Conclusion: 该方法有效提升了WSN数据的互操作性与可重用性，显著降低了网络设置与维护的工作量，为大规模WSN管理提供了可行且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: The paper presents an efficient approach to the semantic enrichment of measured sensor data in Wireless Sensor Networks (WSNs), by bridging techniques from Model-driven Software Development (MDSD) and Semantic Web Technology (SWT). Our approach reinforces data interoperability, fostering data sharing and reuse, by utilizing SWT. Model-based and type-agnostic configuration reduces the overall effort for WSN setup and maintenance, which are traditionally complex and time-consuming tasks. The presented approach addresses the problem of large-scale WSN management through the application of SWT in WSN configuration and management without requiring expert knowledge. Additionally, we present a generic architecture and an implementation which is also supplemented by hands-on descriptions of an illustrative use case. Our experimental results demonstrate that our model-based approach provides non-intrusive semantic enrichment with sub-millisecond computational overhead, as well as partially automated configuration of WSNs.

</details>


### [92] [A Capability Broker for Workflow-Network QoS Coordination in B5G/6G Industrial Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24496)
*Qize Guo,Yan Chen,Taleb Tarik,Bjoern Riemer,Hemant Zope,Hao Yu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了能力代理（Capability Broker）来管理B5G/6G网络中多阶段工作流的QoS承诺生命周期，填补了现有网络控制平面中缺失的协调功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: B5G/6G网络中的工业服务越来越多地以多阶段工作流形式执行，各阶段的QoS需求会发生变化。现有的暴露、分析和策略控制功能虽然可以提供能力信息并执行QoS处理，但无法跟踪工作流与网络之间已接纳的QoS协议，缺少关键的协调功能。

Method: 提出能力代理（Capability Broker），将每个已接纳的轨迹表示为生命周期管理的QoS承诺，包括工作流需求、网络能力、有效时间和执行状态。代理运行在现有暴露和策略控制接口之上，强制执行能力新鲜度、防重复接纳、合法状态转换、按承诺事件排序、恢复路由以及代理-网络状态一致性。

Result: 原型测试（包括微基准测试、故障注入、消融实验和受控消息丢失）表明，完整的代理能以微秒级本地处理开销保持承诺一致性；而移除单个代理功能则会导致重复承诺、过时接纳、非法转换、丢失更新分歧或代理-网络不一致等问题。

Conclusion: 能力代理成功填补了多阶段工作流QoS管理的空白，通过严格的约束机制确保了承诺一致性，为B5G/6G网络中的工业服务提供了可靠的QoS协调功能。

Abstract: Industrial services in beyond-fifth-generation (B5G) and sixth-generation (6G) networks are increasingly executed as multi-phase workflows whose quality-of-service (QoS) demands change across ordered phases. Existing exposure, analytics, and policy-control functions can provide capability information and enforce QoS treatment, but they do not track the admitted QoS agreement between a workflow and the network. This paper studies this missing coordination function in the software control plane. We propose a Capability Broker that represents each admitted trajectory as a lifecycle-managed QoS commitment, including the workflow demand, network capability, validity time, and enforcement state. The Broker operates above existing exposure and policy-control interfaces and enforces capability freshness, duplicate-safe admission, legal state transitions, per-commitment event ordering, recovery routing, and Broker--network state consistency. Prototype results with microbenchmarks, fault injection, ablation, and controlled message loss show that the full Broker preserves commitment consistency with microsecond-level local processing overhead, while removing individual Broker functions exposes duplicate commitments, stale admissions, illegal transitions, lost-update divergences, or Broker-network inconsistency.

</details>


### [93] [ECO-COMM: An Ultra Low-Latency Event Camera based Optical Communication System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24705)
*Chengling Xu,Keigo Hirakawa,Feng Ye*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出ECO-COMM系统，利用事件相机实现超低延迟光通信，设备关联仅需15微秒，符号延迟低至100微秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴下一代应用（如XR、实时控制和分布式传感）对超低延迟通信有迫切需求，传统基于帧的采集方式存在延迟瓶颈。

Method: 利用事件相机的异步感知和微秒级时间分辨率捕获高频光信号；识别并解决时间戳不一致、读出争用、拖尾效应和不应期等硬件挑战；采用八LED发射器和商用事件相机构建原型系统。

Result: 设备关联时间15微秒；符号延迟低至100微秒；32字节负载的端到端延迟低于8毫秒（误码率0.1%）；验证了使用商用硬件实现实用化超低延迟事件相机通信的可行性。

Conclusion: ECO-COMM建立了一种实用的超低延迟通信范式，为对响应性和时间精度要求极高的系统提供了补充性通信方案。

Abstract: Ultralow-latency communication is critical for emerging next-generation applications such as XR, real-time control, and distributed sensing. We present ECO-COMM, an event-camera-based optical communication system for ultra-low-latency device association and lightweight information exchange. By exploiting the asynchronous sensing and microsecond-level temporal resolution of event cameras, ECO-COMM captures high-frequency optical signals without frame-based acquisition delays. We identify and analyze key hardware-induced challenges in event-camera communication, including timestamp inconsistency, readout contention, trailing effects, and the inevitable refractory period, and develop hardware-aware mitigation techniques to address them. Focusing on a single transmitter-receiver optical link, ECO-COMM establishes the feasibility of practical ultra-low-latency event-camera communication using commercially available hardware.A prototype implementation using an eight-LED transmitter and an off-the-shelf event camera achieves device association within 15 microseconds, symbol latency as low as 100 microseconds, and end-to-end latency below 8 milliseconds for 32-byte payloads at 0.1% bit error rate. ECO-COMM establishes a practical and complementary communication paradigm for ultra-low-latency systems where responsiveness and temporal precision are paramount.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [94] [A Survey of Timing Variability in Microservice-Based Software-Defined Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23649)
*Cyrus K. Vattes,Habib Mostafaei,Nirvana Meratnia*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文调研了微服务系统中的时序变异性问题，分析了其来源、传播机制和影响，比较了不同时序模型的优缺点，并以软件定义车辆为例说明局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务系统虽然具有模块化和适应性优势，但其分布式结构使得时序行为难以分析和保证。由于延迟源于服务依赖、资源共享和协调中间件的交互，局部时序扰动可能传播为系统级影响，这促使研究者深入理解时序变异性的本质。

Method: 本综述系统性地调研了微服务系统中时序变异性的来源、传播机制和可观察影响，并以软件定义车辆为驱动示例。通过对比主流时序模型在工作负载稳定性、执行结构、资源共享、可观测性和保证可接受性等方面的假设，总结了各类模型的特点。

Result: 研究识别了微服务系统中时序变异性的主要来源和传播路径，系统性地比较了不同时序模型类别的假设条件和适用范围，揭示了现有模型在微服务架构软件定义车辆场景下的局限性。

Conclusion: 现有时序模型在应对微服务系统的分布式特性、动态工作负载和复杂依赖关系方面存在明显局限，需要开发更适应微服务架构特点的新型时序分析与保证方法，特别是在软件定义车辆等实时性要求严格的应用场景中。

Abstract: Microservice-based systems are modular and adaptable, but their distributed structure makes their timing behavior difficult to analyze and guarantee. Because latency emerges from interactions among service dependencies, shared resources, and coordinating middleware, local timing disturbances can propagate into system-level effects. This survey examines the sources, propagation mechanisms, and observable impacts of timing variability in microservice-based systems, using software-defined vehicles as a motivating example. It compares major classes of timing models by their assumptions about workload stability, execution structure, resource sharing, observability, and guarantee admissibility, and summarizes their limitations within microservice-based software-defined vehicles.

</details>


### [95] [Memory-Sovereign Inference: Output-Exact Execution Beyond Full Residency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23805)
*Lukas Stepanek*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对存储支持的推理系统提出了一个可证伪的验证框架，通过严格分离表示、语义需求、调度请求和流量来识别资源声明的过度夸大问题，并在Qwen3-Next模型上验证了声称的内存边界与实际语义需求之间的矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 存储支持的推理系统容易出现过度声明：进程RSS不包含计入的页面缓存，进程本地设备读数不包括全板使用，且成功生成不能建立正确的异步重用。现有系统缺乏可验证的资源使用证明机制。

Method: 提出可证伪证书框架，分离表示、语义需求、调度请求和流量，同时命名资源权限、精确范围和测试的重用转换。对Qwen3-Next模型进行实验，在32K预填充期间跟踪48×512个层专家对象的语义需求，并与声明的内存边界进行对比验证。

Result: Qwen3-Next的语义需求下界为43.59375 GiB，超过了声明的34 GiB全驻留边界和11 GiB主机硬限制加24 GiB设备边界。缓冲路径达到11 GiB主机上限并记录33,481个memory.max事件。完整的异步组件仅占用阻塞直接路径32.3%的墙钟时间，且具有正向裕度和零限制事件。14个预设控制/故障单元在后续版本中通过验证。

Conclusion: 存储支持推理系统的资源声明经常被过度夸大，需要可证伪的验证框架来准确分离和量化资源使用。LRU64执行能够遵守其主机硬限制/GPU审计契约，而异步组件在保持正确性的同时显著提升了性能。

Abstract: Storage-backed inference is easy to overclaim: process RSS excludes charged page cache, process-local device readings exclude board-wide use, and successful generation does not establish correct asynchronous reuse. We present a falsifiable certificate separating representation, semantic demand, scheduler requests, and traffic while naming resource authorities, exactness horizons, and tested reuse transitions.
  At one Qwen3-Next identity, the stock router selects all 48 x 512 managed layer-expert objects during 32K prefill. Their duplicate-free, overlap-free canonical union gives a 43.59375 GiB semantic-demand lower bound, exceeding the declared 34 GiB full-residency envelope and the 11 GiB host-hard plus physical 24 GiB-device envelope. LRU64 execution stays within its host-hard/GPU-audited contract. Against one prespecified zero-cache oracle, all 64 token IDs, every byte of 64 complete 151,936-float logit rows, 3,408 route events, response bytes, and recorded consumer and destination identities are exact. Recurrent-state and upstream-runtime equality are excluded.
  In a matched source campaign, the buffered path completes exactly but reaches the 11 GiB host ceiling and records 33,481 memory.max events. The blocking one-window direct path and complete eight-window asynchronous component are exact with positive margin and zero limit events. Across six counterbalanced pairs, the complete asynchronous component takes 32.3% of the blocking direct path's wall time at identical physical source bytes per output; the comparison jointly changes queue depth, overlap, and lifecycle implementation. Separately, fourteen prespecified control/fault cells pass across later Qwen3-Next and Gemma 4 binaries, supporting fail-closed behavior only for the named transitions. The principal experiment is one fixed model, workload, runtime, device, and 64-output horizon.

</details>


### [96] [ShardMeter: Sharded and Geo-Distributed Training Without the Guesswork](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23840)
*Tim Beringer,Patrick Diem,Felix Wolf,Arya Mazaheri*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了ShardMeter，一个轻量级分析性能模型，用于预测分片、分布式和去中心化训练下transformer模型端到端运行时间，帮助快速探索配置空间并选择近似最优部署方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模AI模型训练已超出单个数据中心规模，需要跨集群分片训练。然而，资源配置空间巨大，模型大小、GPU内存、批量大小、带宽和分片策略等因素紧密耦合，导致穷尽基准测试和手动调优不切实际。

Method: ShardMeter是一个轻量级分析性能模型，给定模型特征和目标硬件拓扑，估算每个GPU和每个island的吞吐量、训练成本、总挂钟时间，并识别性能瓶颈。支持任意分片、分布式和去中心化训练场景。

Result: 分析揭示了island规模增加时收益递减规律，量化了计算与通信限制扩展之间的转换，评估了超参数权衡，并对大规模去中心化训练进行了成本-吞吐量建模。能够快速探索配置空间并选择近优部署计划。

Conclusion: ShardMeter通过暴露关键性能洞察，帮助快速探索配置空间、选择近似最优部署计划，避免昂贵的试错过程，为大规模分片训练提供了有效的性能预测和优化工具。

Abstract: Training large-scale AI models often outgrows a single data center, demanding sharded, multi-cluster, and decentralized training. However, the huge space of resource allocations makes exhaustive benchmarking and manual tuning impractical, while performance depends on tightly coupled factors like model size, GPU memory, batch size, bandwidth, and sharding strategy. We introduce ShardMeter, a lightweight analytical performance model that predicts the end-to-end runtime of transformer-based workloads across arbitrary sharded, distributed, and even decentralized training. Given a model's characteristics and a target hardware topology, ShardMeter estimates per-GPU and per-island throughput, training cost, total wall-clock time, and identifies performance bottlenecks. Our analysis reveals diminishing-return regimes as island size increases, quantifies transitions between compute- and communication-bound scaling, evaluates hyperparameter trade-offs, and models cost-throughput for large-scale decentralized training. ShardMeter exposes these insights to quickly explore the configuration space, choose near-optimal deployment plans, and avoid costly trial and error.

</details>


### [97] [A Few Shared Random Bits Suffice for Constant-Round Almost Stable Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24102)
*Yijun Chang,Kushagra Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文证明了在一般二分图上，仅需少量共享随机比特即可在常数分布式轮次内求解近似稳定匹配问题，突破了以往算法对有界度或近正则图的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前算法仅在有界度或近正则图上实现常数轮复杂度，而在一般图上轮复杂度与$n$呈多对数依赖。本文旨在突破这一限制，在一般二分图上实现独立于顶点数和最大度数的常数轮近似稳定匹配算法。

Method: 提出度守卫冻结规则（degree-guarded freezing rule），通过单一全局收费论证处理变化剧烈的度数，避免先前工作中使用的Θ(log n)次度阈值迭代。共享随机比特仅用于选择一个公共随机输出迭代。此外，结合低直径分解技术消除对预共享随机性的需求。

Result: 在CONGEST模型下，使用O(log(1/ε))共享随机比特，在O(log(1/ε)/ε^4)轮内计算出期望阻塞对数量至多为ε|E|的匹配。对于任意常数ε>0，轮复杂度为O(1)，独立于顶点数和最大度。无预共享随机性时，轮复杂度为O(log(1/ε)/ε^4 + log n/ε)。在MPC模型下实现O(log(1/ε)/ε^4)轮算法。

Conclusion: 本文首次在一般二分图上实现了常数轮复杂度的近似稳定匹配算法，通过度守卫冻结规则有效处理度数差异，扩展了分布式匹配算法的适用范围，并在CONGEST和MPC模型下均获得了改进的结果。

Abstract: We show that almost stable matching can be solved in constant distributed rounds on general bipartite graphs $G=(V,E)$ using only a few shared random bits. Specifically, in the $\congest$ model, we compute a matching whose expected number of blocking pairs is at most $\varepsilon |E|$ in $O\left(\frac{\log(1/\varepsilon)}{\varepsilon^4}\right)$ rounds using $O\left(\log(1/\varepsilon)\right)$ shared random bits. Thus, for every constant $\varepsilon>0$, the round complexity is $O(1)$, independent of the number of vertices and the maximum degree.
  Previous algorithms achieve constant round complexity only for bounded-degree or almost-regular graphs; on general graphs, their round complexity depends polylogarithmically on $n$. Our main technical idea is a degree-guarded freezing rule that allows widely varying degrees to be handled by a single global charging argument, avoiding the $Θ(\log n)$ successive degree thresholds used in previous work. The shared random bits are used only to select a common random output iteration.
  As consequences, we obtain an $O\left(
  \frac{\log(1/\varepsilon)}{\varepsilon^4}
  +
  \frac{\log n}{\varepsilon} \right)$-round $\congest$ algorithm without pre-shared randomness, via a low-diameter decomposition, and an $O\left(\frac{\log(1/\varepsilon)}{\varepsilon^4}\right)$-round algorithm in the fully-scalable Massively Parallel Computation ($\mpc$) model with linear total memory.

</details>


### [98] [pigzpp: Fast, Parallel, Portable Compression for the Whole Stack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24153)
*Thamme Gowda*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: pigzpp是一个从零开始用C++23重写的并行压缩库，将非线程安全的pigz改造为线程安全的嵌入式库，性能显著提升：zlib-ng后端达2.1倍pigz吞吐量，ISA-L后端达8.5倍，同时保持标准兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: pigz虽然广泛部署，但其进程全局可变状态导致它不是可重入的、可直接嵌入的库。需要将其设计改造为线程安全的库，以支持C++、Python、WebAssembly、Go和Rust等多语言环境。

Method: 从零开始使用C++23重写，将设计转换为线程安全库；暴露一个加速的DEFLATE核心给多语言接口；提供两种后端：便携式zlib-ng后端和x86-64专用的Intel ISA-L快速路径；在自动化兼容性测试下进行AI辅助的现代化改造。

Result: zlib-ng后端在文本语料库上达到pigz CLI吞吐量的2.1倍、Python内存gzip的16倍；ISA-L后端分别达到8.5倍和50倍。ISA-L在level 6配置下输出约大10%，但输出仍符合标准，可跨解码于gzip、pigz和unzip。

Conclusion: 成功创建了一个自包含、多平台的压缩栈，证明了AI辅助现代化改造的可行性，同时提供了多入口ZIP档案和快速PNG编解码器等应用层便利功能。

Abstract: pigz is a widely deployed parallel gzip utility, but its process-global mutable state means that it was not designed as a reentrant, directly embeddable library. pigzpp is a from-scratch C++23 rewrite that turns the design into a thread-safe library with one accelerated DEFLATE core exposed to C++, Python, WebAssembly, Go, and Rust. As application-level conveniences built on that same core, it also provides multi-entry ZIP archives and a fast PNG codec. Its portable zlib-ng backend preserves gzip/zlib's ratio; its Intel ISA-L backend is an x86-64-only fast path that produces about 10% larger output at level 6 on our text corpus. In that configuration, zlib-ng reaches 2.1 times the CLI throughput of pigz and 16 times that of Python's in-memory gzip; ISA-L reaches 8.5 times and 50 times, respectively. Outputs remain standards-compliant and cross-decode with gzip, pigz, and unzip. The result is a self-contained, multi-platform compression stack and a case study in AI-assisted modernization under automated compatibility tests.

</details>


### [99] [An HPC Approach to Accelerate Tensor Decompositions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24307)
*Markus Hellgren,Erna Begovic Kovac,Hans O. Karlsson,Roman Iakymchuk*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种新型Jacobi型张量分解算法并开发了支持任意阶张量的CUDA实现，在NVIDIA H100 GPU上实现了比MATLAB参考实现快两个数量级的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子系统复杂度随规模急剧增长，即使是中等规模的模型也难以模拟，因此迫切需要能够处理高维数据（张量）的高效方法。

Method: 研究了一种新型Jacobi型张量分解算法，并开发了基于CUDA的算法实现，支持在单个GPU上处理任意阶张量。

Result: 在NVIDIA H100 GPU上测试表明，该算法对高达九维的可对角化张量能正确收敛，运行时间随张量阶数增长呈可预测的扩展性，且总体性能比原始MATLAB参考实现提高超过两个数量级。

Conclusion: 该CUDA实现成功验证了Jacobi型张量分解算法在高维情况下的有效性和可扩展性，显著提升了计算效率，为处理高维量子系统模拟提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Quantum systems grow in complexity so rapidly that even modest models become difficult to simulate, creating a strong need for methods that can handle high-dimensional data, also known as tensors. In this work, we investigate a novel Jacobi-type tensor algorithm for tensor decomposition and develop a CUDA-based algorithm that supports tensors of arbitrary order on a single GPU. We test the implementation on NVIDIA H100 GPUs and show that the algorithm converges correctly for diagonalizable tensors up to nine dimensions, with runtime scaling in a predictable way as tensor order grows. Finally, our general algorithm outperforms the original MATLAB reference by more than two orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [100] [Mixed-Precision SEM-Based CFD Simulations on GPUs: A Taylor-Green Vortex case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24348)
*Yanxiang Chen,Manuel Münsch,Roman Iakymchuk*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对基于谱元法的矩阵无关CFD求解器Neko，提出了三层分层混合精度控制模型。通过识别精度敏感组件，在fp32边界环境下实现了约34%的时间和能耗降低，同时提高了鲁棒性。研究结果表明混合精度应被视为仿真级控制问题，而非仅限于Krylov求解器优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合精度方法有望降低计算流体动力学仿真的计算成本和能耗，但其有效性很大程度上取决于在仿真流水线中何处降低精度。传统的混合精度优化主要集中在Krylov求解器层面，但性能分析显示流体时间步长并非仅由Krylov收敛主导，速度和压力求解器每步只需少量迭代，而大量运行时间消耗在其他SEM算子和求解器组件中。

Method: 使用Neko求解器研究Taylor-Green涡流案例，通过性能分析识别运行时瓶颈，提出了三层分层混合精度控制模型。在fp64和fp32精度边界下分别评估两组配置：fp64边界组识别精度敏感组件；fp32边界组提供实际效益。同时探索了针对性的fp16核函数覆盖策略。

Result: 对于研究的高雷诺数案例，在fp32边界组中选定的配置相对于fp64基线减少了约34%的求解时间和能耗，同时相比全局fp32提高了鲁棒性。fp64边界组识别出精度敏感组件，表明面向SEM的fp32计算是未来优化的有前景方向。针对性的fp16核函数覆盖显示出对选定操作的潜力，但在基于梯度的量（如enstrophy）中增加了敏感性。

Conclusion: 基于矩阵无关SEM的CFD混合精度应被视为仿真级控制问题，而非仅作为Krylov求解器优化。三层分层混合精度控制模型能够有效平衡计算效率、能耗和数值精度，为CFD仿真的混合精度优化提供了新的系统性方法。

Abstract: Mixed precision is a promising approach for reducing the computational cost and energy consumption of Computation Fluid Dynamics (CFD) simulations, but its effectiveness depends strongly on where precision is reduced within the full simulation pipeline. In this work, we study Taylor-Green vortex case using Neko, a matrix-free CFD solver based on the spectral element method (SEM). Profiling shows that the fluid time step is not dominated by Krylov convergence alone: the velocity and pressure solvers require only a small number of iterations per step, while a substantial fraction of runtime is spent in other SEM operators and solver components. Motivated by this structure, we propose a three-level hierarchical mixed-precision control model. Two groups of configurations are evaluated in environments bounded by 64-bit floating-point (fp64) and 32-bit floating-point (fp32) precision, respectively. The fp64-bounded group identifies accuracy sensitive components and shows that SEM-focused fp32 computation is a promising direction for future optimization. The fp32-bounded group provides the main practical benefit. For the high Reynolds number case studied, selected configurations reduce both time- and energy-to-solution by about 34% relative to the fp64 baseline, while improving robustness compared with global fp32. Targeted fp16 kernel overrides are also explored, showing potential for selected operations but increased sensitivity in gradient-based quantities such as enstrophy. Overall, these results indicate that mixed-precision for matrix-free SEM-based CFD should be treated as a simulation-level control problem rather than solely as a Krylov-solver optimization.

</details>


### [101] [SatDL: Jointly Optimizing Data Redistribution and Training for Satellite-Based Distributed Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24516)
*Hao Wu,Kin Whye Chew,Yizhan Han,Han Li,Jingxian Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了SatDL框架，通过优化数据重分配策略，有效解决了卫星分布式学习中标签不平衡导致的训练收敛慢和能耗高的问题，显著减少了总学习时间和能量消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星分布式学习面临严重的非独立同分布数据（标签不平衡）问题，因为每颗卫星观测不同地理区域导致标签分布差异。这种不平衡延长了训练时间并增加了太阳能卫星的能耗。现有方法要么完全重分配数据（通信延迟大），要么修改本地学习算法（训练时间仍然很长），两种极端都会导致总端到端学习时间过长和能耗增加。

Method: 提出SatDL数据重分配框架，核心是开发了一个Distributor-Critic框架，联合建模和优化数据传输延迟与训练时间，在最小化总端到端学习时间的同时平衡通信开销和训练效率。

Result: 通过1,584颗Starlink卫星星座的仿真和NVIDIA Jetson/A100 GPU硬件仿真，在五个数据集上的实验表明：SatDL将总端到端学习时间减少了高达18.6%，机载能耗降低12.23-88.00%，同时推理准确率与最先进基线保持在几个百分点以内。

Conclusion: SatDL成功解决了卫星分布式学习中标签不平衡带来的效率与能耗挑战，在保持模型性能的同时显著优化了总学习时间和能量消耗，为卫星在轨机器学习提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Satellite-based distributed learning promises to train machine-learning models directly in orbit using massive, globally dispersed sensor data, thereby avoiding large-scale data downloads to ground servers. However, training convergence is significantly slowed by severe non-IID data, specifically label imbalance, as each satellite observes different geographic regions with distinct labels. This imbalance extends training duration and increases energy consumption for solar-powered satellites. Existing approaches either fully redistribute data to enforce IID conditions - accelerating convergence but incurring substantial communication delays - or avoid redistribution entirely by modifying local learning algorithms to mitigate the impact of label imbalance, which, however, still prolong training and increase energy use. Both extremes result in excessive total end-to-end learning time (data-transfer delay plus training time) and thus elevated onboard energy consumption.
  We present SatDL, a data-redistribution framework designed to minimize total end-to-end learning time. At its core, SatDL develops a Distributor-Critic framework that jointly models and optimizes data-transfer delay and training time. Evaluations through trace-driven simulations of a 1,584-satellite Starlink constellation and hardware emulations using NVIDIA Jetson and A100 GPUs across five datasets show SatDL reduces total end-to-end learning time by up to 18.6% and onboard energy consumption by 12.23-88.00%, while maintaining inference accuracy within a few percentage points of state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [102] [Scalable datacenter replication with mostly-synchronous consensus on hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24622)
*Davide Rovelli,Philipp Berdesinski,Rodrigo Otoni,Patrick Eugster*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了scarHW，一种基于FPGA智能网卡的硬件可扩展复制方案，通过新型POPCON协作共识算法，在保证一致性的前提下实现高扩展性，相比现有方案吞吐和延迟提升两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式系统中的一致性数据复制（涉及共识问题）代价高昂且难以扩展，特别影响对性能要求严格的数据中心服务。现有系统要么规模小，要么需要放松一致性保证。

Method: 设计并实现scarHW网络卡，核心为POPCON共识算法，部署在FPGA智能NIC上。该算法利用数据中心可编程网络设备的"主要同步"特性，采用协作共识变体允许多个同时决策，形式化规范并使用TLA+验证。

Result: FPGA原型系统应用于Redis和Zookeeper服务，吞吐和延迟相比最先进方案提升最多两个数量级。在少数副本故障时实现零停机，提供高鲁棒、线速、可扩展的复制系统。

Conclusion: scarHW成功解决了一致性复制的可扩展性问题，在保证安全性和可用性的同时实现了显著的性能提升，为数据中心服务提供了可靠的硬件级复制解决方案。

Abstract: Consistent replication of data among distributed processes -- a task involving the well-known consensus problem -- is notoriously expensive and hard to scale, affecting especially datacenter services with stringent performance requirements. To mitigate this problem, we introduce scalable replication in-hardware ( scarHW ): a network card design that improves throughput and latency of consistent replication even when increasing the number of replicas, whereas current systems operate at a small scale or with relaxed consistency guarantees. At the heart of scarHW is our novel POPUC consensus algorithm, implemented in an FPGA smartNIC to take full advantage of the "mostly synchronous" behavior of programmable network devices in the datacenter. Unlike widely-adopted "mostly asynchronous" coordination protocols such as Paxos or leaderless alternatives, POPUC implements a generalized variant of consensus dubbed collaborative consensus which allows for several simultaneous decisions, achieving great scalability without compromising availability. POPUC preserves safety guarantees in the presence of process crash-stop and message send/receive omission failures (capturing incidental asynchrony) and has been formally specified and verified in TLA+. Our FPGA prototype improves throughput and latency of widely-used services Redis and Zookeeper by up to two orders of magnitude compared to the state of the art. scarHW-based services also achieve zero downtime upon failure of a minority of replicas, offering a highly-robust, wire-speed, scalable replication system.

</details>


### [103] [Asynchronous Verifiable Information Dispersal with Low Space and Communication Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24636)
*Thomas Locher,Yvonne-Anne Pignolet*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效的异步可验证信息分散协议，通过二维矩阵编码和定制化分散算法，在存储、分散、检索和恢复四个维度上实现了低复杂度，并在保持检索通信复杂度最优的同时，显著提升了节点恢复的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异步可验证信息分散协议虽然能够在拜占庭环境下实现最优的存储空间复杂度和数据检索通信复杂度，但数据分散和节点恢复这两个关键操作的研究相对不足，缺乏对这些操作的全面优化。

Method: 提出了一种新的机制将数据编码为二维矩阵，并设计了定制的分散算法。协议的核心是将数据在二维结构中进行编码，从而优化分散、存储、检索和恢复各个阶段的性能。此外，还提出了协议的变体，通过增加检索和恢复的通信复杂度来降低空间复杂度和分散的通信复杂度。

Result: 在保持检索通信复杂度最优的同时，显著改善了节点恢复的性能，突破了现有技术的局限。协议变体展示了在不同性能指标之间的权衡能力，能够在空间复杂度、分散通信复杂度与检索恢复通信复杂度之间进行灵活调整。

Conclusion: 所提出的协议在所有考虑的指标（分散、存储、检索和恢复复杂度）之间达到了良好的平衡，适用于广泛的现实世界应用场景，为分布式存储系统提供了更全面的解决方案。

Abstract: The primary goal of a distributed storage system is to ensure that clients can both write and read data in a reliable and consistent manner, even in the presence of failures. While existing asynchronous verifiable information dispersal (AVID) protocols achieve optimal space complexity for storage and communication complexity for data retrieval in a Byzantine setting, the crucial operations of data dispersal and node recovery have received less attention.
  We propose an efficient AVID protocol that simultaneously guarantees low complexities for dispersal, storage, retrieval, and recovery. At the core of the proposed protocol lies a novel mechanism to encode data in a two-dimensional matrix and a bespoke dispersal algorithm. The protocol maintains an optimal communication complexity for retrieval while substantially improving upon the state of the art for recovery. Additionally, we describe how the protocol can be modified in order to reduce the space complexity and the communication complexity for dispersal, at the expense of a higher communication complexity for retrieval and recovery. As the proposed protocols strike a balance across all considered metrics, they are suitable for a broad range of real-world use cases.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [104] [RENDER: Controlling Reader-Facing Evidence in LLM Memory Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23568)
*Yuan Si,Simeng Han,Daming Li,Jialu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 引入RENDER基准测试，控制对话历史不变而改变呈现格式，证明记忆和RAG系统的评估结果显著受输入格式（如ChatGPT风格、摘要、结构化记录、原始对话）影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆和RAG评估常忽略回答模型的输入格式细节，但同一对话历史可呈现为记忆条目、摘要、类型化记录或原始摘录，这种差异可能导致评估结果产生偏差。

Method: 提出RENDER基准控制方法：使用五级包阶梯定位答案相关内容进入输入的位置；结合确定性模板模拟ChatGPT风格条目、LangChain摘要、MemGPT类型化记录和原始对话；在500个LongMemEval问题和9个模型上进行测试。

Result: 匹配预算的解析包比近因截断的原始对话高42.4-72.6分；部署风格模板中最佳-最差差距24.6-48.8分；7/9模型上ChatGPT风格条目优于原始对话；三个在形式化账本包上0%的模型从自然语言条目中达到45.4-53.4%准确率；效应在检索噪声下持续存在并迁移至HotpotQA。

Conclusion: 记忆和RAG评估应报告或控制面向读者的输入格式，因为呈现格式显著影响模型性能，这为系统设计和评估方法提供了重要启示。

Abstract: Memory and RAG evaluations often treat the answering model's input as an implementation detail, even though systems may render the same history as a memory entry, summary, typed record, or raw excerpt. We introduce RENDER, a benchmark control that fixes the conversation while varying the reader-facing artifact. RENDER combines a five-level packet ladder, localizing when answer-bearing content enters the input, with deterministic templates approximating ChatGPT-style entries, LangChain summaries, MemGPT-style typed records, and raw conversation. On 500 LongMemEval questions and nine models, matched-budget resolved packets beat recency-truncated raw dialogue by 42.4-72.6 points. In deployed-style templates, best-worst spread is 24.6-48.8 points per model; under the primary scorer, ChatGPT-style entries have higher point estimates than raw conversation on 7 of 9 models. Judge rescoring preserves the positive aggregate effect, but model-specific significance is mixed. Three models scoring 0 percent on formal ledger packets answer the same facts from natural-language entries at 45.4-53.4 percent. The effect persists under retrieval noise and transfers to HotpotQA, suggesting that memory/RAG evaluations should report or control the reader-facing artifact.

</details>


### [105] [ESQ-Bench: A Multi-Tier Enterprise Oracle Benchmark for Evaluating NL2SQL Dialect Generalization and Silent Semantic Divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23569)
*Sanjay Mishra,Divya Chukkapalli,Ganesh R. Naik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文引入ESQ-Bench，首个面向企业级Oracle数据库的NL2SQL基准测试，包含不同复杂度层次和静默偏差评估，揭示了现有模型在企业级模式上的性能显著下降问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NL2SQL基准测试（如Spider和BIRD）依赖简化的学术模式和开源SQL方言，无法反映企业数据库环境的复杂性，导致模型在真实企业环境中的性能被高估。

Method: 构建ESQ-Bench基准：1) 创建六个填充模式（465表，164,682行），在Oracle、PostgreSQL、MySQL和SQL Server上部署相同种子数据；2) 设计三个企业模式复杂度层次；3) 开发四指标评估框架（EM、EX、SR、SD）；4) 生成550个黄金验证的问题-查询对；5) 采用模式链接提示对GPT-4o、Claude Sonnet 4.6和Llama 3.2进行测试。

Result: GPT-4o模式链接EX随复杂度层次递减（79.8%→60.3%→57.2%），EM全层次低于7%，EX通过的查询中73-99%存在操作静默偏差。Claude Sonnet 4.6在各层次均超越GPT-4o（87.4%、74.9%、68.7%）。GPT-4o零样本因执行率低和幸存者偏差在层次2-3反超模式链接。本地Llama 3.2仅达13.3%银行级EX，凸显开源与闭源API模型在企业Oracle模式上的巨大差距。

Conclusion: 现有NL2SQL模型在企业级数据库环境中的性能显著下降，复杂模式导致严重的静默偏差问题（正确结果但语义错误），闭源API模型与开源权重基线之间存在显著差距，亟需针对企业级复杂场景的模型优化。

Abstract: State-of-the-art Natural Language to SQL (NL2SQL) models report execution accuracy exceeding 89 percent on established benchmarks such as Spider and BIRD. However, these benchmarks rely on simplified academic schemas and open-source SQL dialects that do not reflect the complexity of enterprise database environments. We introduce ESQ-Bench, an Oracle-first NL2SQL benchmark with systematic complexity tiers and silent-divergence evaluation across three enterprise schema complexity tiers. We constructed and released six populated schemas (465 tables, 164,682 rows, zero empty tables) with identical seed data on Oracle, PostgreSQL, MySQL, and SQL Server, a four-metric evaluation harness (EM, EX, SR, SD), and 550 gold-validated question-query pairs (Tier-1: 95; Tier-2: 228; Tier-3: 227). Schema-linked prompting with GPT-4o shows monotonic execution-match degradation across tiers: 79.8, 60.3, and 57.2 percent EX on executed queries (June 2026), versus 75.6, 80.4, and 95.8 percent on an earlier 142-question pilot slice. EM stays below 7 percent tier-wide; operational silent-divergence reaches 73 to 99 percent among EX-passing queries. Failure analysis shows wrong-result semantics dominate at higher tiers. Claude Sonnet 4.6 with schema-linked prompts reaches 87.4, 74.9, and 68.7 percent EX (executed queries), exceeding GPT-4o schema-linked on every tier. GPT-4o zero-shot EX on executed queries (78.7, 73.5, and 77.8 percent) inverts schema-linked at Tiers 2 to 3 due to lower execution rates and survivor bias in the zero-shot versus schema-linked analysis. Local Llama 3.2 schema-linked reaches only 13.3 percent bank-wide EX (73 out of 550), underscoring the gap between closed API models and open-weight baselines on enterprise Oracle schemas.

</details>


### [106] [LLM Agents Perform Controlled Experiments Using Simulation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23622)
*Yuchen Xia,Michael Weyrich,Nasser Jazdi,Johannes Stümpfle,Johannes Sigel,Akshay Narla,Gavin K. Reynolds,Anna Jawor-Baczynska,Pol Llopart*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个多智能体框架，使大语言模型能够与药物工艺设计的科学仿真模型结合，通过受控实验进行干预推理，从而产生比纯语言推理更具可操作性的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在推理、规划和工具使用方面表现出色，但许多科学和工程任务需要理解系统如何响应干预，而这依赖于受控实验。纯文本和代码生成无法满足科学工程任务中对系统干预响应的需求。

Method: 提出了一个多智能体框架，将LLM智能体与高保真仿真模型耦合。系统根据用户查询和基线配置，构建结构化任务表示，设计实验，执行比较仿真，解释结果，并综合证据提出工艺参数优化建议，支持通过干预、比较和观察进行推理。

Result: 在工业应用场景中，该系统相比纯语言推理方法产生了更高的输出特异性，用户评价的正确性和有用性均得到提升。消融研究和可视化案例分析进一步验证了仿真集成实验推理的有效性和实用价值。

Conclusion: 通过将语言模型与高保真仿真模型在交互式智能体框架中结合，系统能够支持基于干预、比较和观察的推理过程，从而为科学工程任务产生更具体、更具可操作性的输出。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong capabilities in reasoning, planning, and tool use, but many scientific and engineering tasks require more than plausible text and code generation. They require understanding how a system responds to intervention, which in practice depends on controlled experimentation. In this work, we propose a multi-agent framework that enables LLM agents to conduct controlled experiments with scientific simulation models for pharmaceutical process design. Given a user query and a baseline configuration, the system constructs a structured task representation, designs experiments, executes comparative simulation, interprets the resulting outcomes, and synthesizes evidence-based recommendations for process parameter optimization. By coupling language models with high-fidelity simulation models in an interactive agent framework, the proposed system supports reasoning through intervention, comparison, and observation. As a result, it produces more specific and actionable outputs than language-only reasoning. In an industrial application setting, this advantage is reflected in higher output specificity as well as improved user-rated correctness and helpfulness. Ablation studies and visualized case analyses further demonstrate the effectiveness and practical utility of simulation-integrated experimental reasoning.

</details>


### [107] [A survey detection channel overrides the pixels in an astronomical foundation model, and biases tomographic mean redshifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23626)
*Ihor Kendiukhov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 天文学基础模型会从训练数据中继承星表的不完整性，导致系统性偏差；通过因果干预发现模型主要依赖检测通道而非实际光源，建议移除检测通道以消除偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天文学基础模型通常在巡天图像像素和由这些像素衍生的星表产品上联合训练，但这些星表存在可测量的不完整性。研究者担心模型会继承这种不完整性作为系统性偏差，因此需要审计模型是否确实存在这一问题及其影响程度。

Method: 对AION-1（一个在2亿多个天体上训练的39模态Transformer模型）进行因果干预实验。通过保持图像token字节相同，仅编辑巡天分割图（segmentation map），测试模型对通量、尺寸、椭率、红移等物理量的报告变化。还分析了Legacy Survey管道的缺失率（3.68%），并评估其对层析平均红移的影响。

Result: 发现模型存在检测门控机制：当仅改变分割图时，模型报告的物理量变化是安慰剂组的110-4400倍；模型依赖源在场中心的存在性（r=0.47）而非掩模所包围的光（r=0.30），对322个真实混合源忽略了管道对光的分配（R=-0.006）。矛盾的光度数据使模型效果比完全不提供元数据差9倍。层析平均红移的中位数偏移达到LSST DESC要求的0.71倍，在40个分配中有12个超过要求，最差情况达到8.3倍。光谱数据或移除检测通道可消除该效应。

Conclusion: 天文学基础模型会从训练数据中继承星表的不完整性，形成系统性偏差。这一问题源于模型的检测门控机制和分词器的局限性（图像编解码器仅有28个有效状态）。解决方案包括移除检测通道（无性能损失）或使用光谱数据。稀疏字典作为因果工具不可靠，恢复率仅26-75%。这为未来天文学基础模型的设计提供了重要警示。

Abstract: Foundation models for astronomy are trained on survey pixels together with the catalogue products derived from those pixels. Those catalogues are incomplete at a measurable rate, and a model trained on both inherits that incompleteness as a systematic. We audit AION-1, a 39-modality transformer trained on more than 200 million objects, using causal interventions on its inputs.
  Holding the image tokens byte-identical and editing only the survey segmentation map changes every quantity the model reports -- flux, size, ellipticity, redshift -- by 110-4400 times a matched placebo. The mechanism is detection gating, presence at the field centre (r = 0.47), not the light the mask encloses (r = 0.30); across 322 real blends the model ignores how the pipeline partitioned the light (R = -0.006). Nor is the preference specific to that channel: contradicted catalogue photometry leaves the model nine times worse than supplying no metadata at all.
  The Legacy Survey pipeline leaves 3.68% of targets with no segment covering their position. Propagating that rate, with a miss represented by the fields the pipeline actually returns, shifts tomographic mean redshifts by a median 0.71 times the LSST DESC requirement over 40 assignments and exceeds it in 12; observed positional errors take the worst bin to 8.3 times. Drawing the misses by their measured magnitude dependence rather than uniformly does not change it. Spectroscopy removes the effect, withholding the detection channel removes it at no measurable cost, and the effect grows with model scale.
  Two further limits lie in the tokeniser: its image codec resolves 28 effective states on source patches against 934 for the spectrum codec, and the redshift readout is quantisation-limited. Sparse dictionaries are unreliable causal handles: across 15, recovery spans 26-75% and moves up to 18 points on the seed alone.

</details>


### [108] [TRACE: Transition-Aware Residual Control for Multi-Objective Materials Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23631)
*Kang Zhou,Yujia Tong,Yong Tao,Jingling Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TRACE框架，通过记录材料编辑操作引起的性质变化来指导多目标材料发现，相比现有方法提高了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多目标材料发现中的LLM智能体仅存储已评估的候选材料及其得分，而忽略了哪些可执行编辑导致了有用的性质变化。当目标之间存在竞争关系时，改进某一性质的编辑可能损害另一性质，这使得局部优化变得困难。

Method: TRACE是一个转移感知残差控制框架，将已评估的编辑作为反馈的基本单元。该框架将每次局部优化记录为父节点-编辑-子节点的转移，并观测性质变化量；聚合转移证据以估计可复用的编辑效果；根据预测的编辑能力对未来编辑进行排序，以减少当前候选材料的约束违规，同时避免损害已满足的目标。

Result: 在受控的相同骨干网络比较中，TRACE优于最先进的LLM智能体基线LLEMA，将宏平均命中率从18.13%提升至25.96%。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模编辑操作与性质变化之间的关系，TRACE有效解决了多目标优化中目标竞争导致的局部优化困难问题，为材料发现领域提供了更高效的搜索策略。

Abstract: Multi-objective materials discovery with LLM agents is often limited not only by how many candidates can be proposed, but by how effectively each costly property evaluation informs the next search step. Existing agents mainly store evaluated candidates and their scores, so they know which materials succeeded but not which executable edits caused useful property changes. This makes local refinement difficult when objectives compete and an edit that improves one property may damage another. We propose TRACE, a transition-aware residual control framework that treats evaluated edits as the basic unit of feedback. TRACE records each local refinement as a parent-edit-child transition with observed property deltas, aggregates transition evidence to estimate reusable edit effects, and ranks future edits by their predicted ability to reduce the current candidate's remaining constraint violations while avoiding damage to already satisfied objectives. In a controlled same-backbone comparison, TRACE improves over LLEMA, the state-of-the-art LLM-agent baseline, raising macro-average hit rate from 18.13\% to 25.96\%.

</details>


### [109] [Autonomous Mathematical Discovery in an Open-World Multi-Agent Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23691)
*Stephen Chung,Wenyu Du,William J. Wesley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了Station环境中AI智能体的自主数学发现能力，该环境是一个去中心化的多智能体协作平台。实验显示，在多个数学问题上获得了相对于既有文献的创新成果，包括新的有限域Kakeya集合、高维接触配置、以及多个著名数学问题的改进结果，并提供了定理分析和验证材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索AI智能体在没有中央协调器或预定义流程的情况下，能够自主地、协作地进行数学发现的能力。研究的目标是建立一个开放的、多智能体协作环境，让不同模型家族的AI智能体能够自主选择研究方向、执行实验、进行协作，并构建共享的科学文献库。

Method: 构建了一个名为Station的开放世界多智能体环境，AI智能体来自不同模型家族，在没有中央协调器或预设流程的情况下自主工作。智能体自主选择研究方向、执行实验、相互协作，并构建共享的科学文献。研究涵盖AlphaEvolve目录中的12个构造问题和两个额外案例研究。智能体不仅生成数值构造，还提供定理和分析来解释这些构造的原理。

Result: 在五个问题上获得了相对于先前文献的创新成果：（1）发现了一个新的有限域Kakeya集合的无限族；（2）在11维空间中发现了新的精确604点接触配置；（3）为离散化Kakeya针问题和符号不确定性问题创造了新纪录；（4）显著改进了Erdős最小重叠问题的下界；（5）发现了Book Ramsey数的新的无限族。重要的是，智能体不仅产生了数值构造，还产生了定理和分析来解释这些构造如何工作，使得结果更具可解释性且便于数学家在此基础上进行后续研究。

Conclusion: 研究证明了AI智能体在去中心化多智能体环境中具有自主进行数学发现的能力，能够产生新颖的、具有科学价值的数学成果。智能体能够同时生成构造结果和理论解释，这提高了结果的可解释性和可用性。研究团队公开发布了所有原始智能体对话、证明和验证代码，为这些发现的产生过程提供了透明记录。

Abstract: We study autonomous mathematical discovery in the Station, an open-world multi-agent environment in which AI agents from different model families pursue a shared research goal without a central coordinator or scripted pipeline. Agents choose their own research directions, conduct experiments, collaborate, and build a shared scientific literature. Across 12 construction problems from the AlphaEvolve catalogue and two additional case studies, the Station obtained results novel relative to the prior literature on five problems: a new infinite family of finite-field Kakeya sets, new exact 604-point kissing configurations in dimension 11, new records for the discretized Kakeya needle and sign uncertainty problems, and a substantially improved lower bound for Erdős's minimum-overlap problem. Agents also discovered novel infinite families for Book Ramsey numbers. Importantly, the agents produced not only numerical constructions but also theorems and analyses explaining how those constructions work, making the results more interpretable and easier for mathematicians to build upon. We release all raw agent dialogues, proofs, and verification code, providing a transparent record of how these discoveries emerged.

</details>


### [110] [Function-Level Execution Feedback for Code Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23632)
*Idris Nechnech,Sehwan Kim,Jimin Seo,Yeongoon Kim,Minhae Oh,Sangwoo Hong,Jungwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出STEP-KTODER框架，通过模块级函数步骤定义和单元测试自动标注实现代码生成的过程监督，显著优于传统结果级偏好优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过程监督在数学推理任务中已取得成功，但在代码生成领域尚未充分探索，主要原因是代码生成缺乏标准的"步骤"概念。现有监督方法可针对代码行、推理轨迹或程序状态，导致标注目标不明确，难以有效实施过程监督。

Method: 提出STEP-KTODER框架：1) 将分解后的多函数程序中的模块级函数定义为步骤；2) 通过自动生成单元测试为每个函数步骤分配二元正确性标签；3) 将函数级过程监督与完整程序的结果级反馈相结合，构建代码特定的逐步KTO（Kahneman-Tversky Optimization）偏好优化方法。

Result: 在HumanEval(+)、MBPP(+)、BigCodeBench和LiveCodeBench四个基准数据集上，STEP-KTODER均优于仅使用结果反馈的KTO和DPO方法。进一步分析表明，基于执行的标签至关重要：LLM作为评判者的标注会系统性地过度预测函数失败，破坏正向步骤标签质量，导致下游偏好优化性能下降。

Conclusion: 代码生成中的过程监督可通过模块级函数步骤定义和自动化单元测试实现有效实施。基于执行的正确性标签优于LLM评判者标注，能够提升代码偏好优化性能，为代码生成任务提供了一种可扩展的过程监督方案。

Abstract: Process supervision has improved mathematical reasoning, where intermediate steps are naturally expressed as chains of thought. In code generation, however, process supervision remains underexplored because there is no standard notion of a step. Supervision can target lines, reasoning traces, or program states, making it unclear what to label and optimize.
