We close this gap with a medical perturbation audit: a 30-operator battery edits both the chain and the question with clinically motivated operators (severity reversal, negation flip, demographic swap, evidence ablation), paired with a chain-update times answer-flip joint analysis that classifies each model by its failure mode. Applied to 14 LLMs on four medical QA benchmarks, three independent tests converge: the Chain-Decoupling Rate (CDR; chain does not register the edit and the answer does not flip) is 72.9% panel-wide on clinically meaningful destructive edits, chain corruption leaves accuracy unchanged, and removing CoT prompting does not reduce accuracy. Two board-certified clinicians re-annotate N=197 perturbed questions; 98.5% leave the gold defensible. The pattern holds across medical and reasoning fine-tuning and scale; on the closed-source tier, where the chain text is unavailable, the answer-side signals are consistent with the same decoupling. Our framework and CDR provide a reusable yardstick for auditing whether medical CoT is faithful or merely documentation.

</details>


### [213] [CAFE: Self-Improving Search Agents Need Co-Evolving Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24794)
*Boyang Liu,Senjie Jin,Peixin Wang,Zhangyue Yin,Yibo Wang,Yuhao Zhou,Xinbing Liang,Shizheng Zhu,Yuhui Wang,Jingqi Tong,Zhiheng Xi,Jiazheng Zhang,Clive Bai,Clarenceai,Blaze Chen,Tao Gui,Qi Zhang,Xuanjing Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CAFE框架，通过共享参数模型交替扮演搜索智能体和批评者角色，实现反馈与策略的协同进化，在多个智能体搜索基准上超越现有RL方法并减少幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的结果监督搜索智能体虽然能学习何时及如何检索证据，但终端奖励无法定位中间错误，也无法在错误累积前重定向轨迹。需要一种机制来提供及时的纠正性反馈，使智能体能够在搜索过程中进行错误修正。

Method: CAFE框架采用共享参数模型交替扮演搜索智能体和批评者角色。初始化时从基础智能体失败轨迹构建反馈条件恢复机制。在线RL阶段，使用提示级调用-跳过成功差距塑造反馈请求回报，并通过反馈感知优势重塑调整反馈前后的token优势。离线阶段，通过匹配成功和失败轨迹的rollout衍生偏好优化学习反馈。

Result: 在七个智能体搜索基准上，CAFE平均表现优于评估的基于RL的搜索智能体，在六个分布外基准上保持增益，并减少了答案级别的幻觉。消融实验表明，仅改进智能体或批评者最终会达到瓶颈，而交替更新两者能持续提升性能。

Conclusion: 自改进的搜索智能体需要能够与其所引导的策略协同进化的反馈，单一角色的改进存在局限性，智能体与批评者的耦合进化是提升性能的关键。

Abstract: Outcome-supervised search agents learn when and how to retrieve evidence, but terminal rewards neither localize intermediate errors nor redirect an ongoing trajectory before those errors compound. Treating corrective feedback as a learned in-trajectory intervention couples the two roles: the agent must decide when to request and use feedback, while the critic must infer useful corrections from outcome-confounded rollouts whose failure patterns shift as the agent improves. We introduce CAFE (Coupled Agent--Feedback Evolution), a framework in which a shared-parameter model alternates between search-agent and critic roles. CAFE initializes feedback-conditioned recovery from trajectories built around the base agent's own failures, then couples online and offline optimization. During online RL, a comparative feedback estimate uses a prompt-level call--skip success gap to shape request returns, while feedback-aware advantage shaping reweights token advantages before and after feedback. Offline, rollout-derived preference optimization learns feedback from matched successful and unsuccessful trajectories. On seven agentic search benchmarks, CAFE outperforms the evaluated RL-based search agents on average, retains its gains across all six out-of-domain benchmarks, and reduces answer-level hallucinations. One-sided ablations show that improving only the agent or only the critic eventually plateaus, whereas alternating the two updates continues to improve performance. These findings suggest that a self-improving search agent needs feedback that co-evolves with the policy it guides.

</details>


### [214] [StarHarness: Evolving Harnesses with Stratified Search for Enterprise Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24804)
*Esakkivel Esakkiraja,Denis Akhiyarov,Vikas Yadav,Sai Rajeswar,Patrice Bechard,Sridhar Nemala,Sagar Davasam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了StarHarness框架，通过进化环境特定的智能体外壳（包括提示、工具接口、技能、子代理结构等），在保持模型权重固定的前提下，将三个企业任务基准测试的性能提升20-35个百分点，且改进在未见任务和跨模型迁移中持续有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在工具丰富的企业任务中，预训练模型与环境之间存在持久的失配问题，导致性能不佳。传统方法通过微调模型权重来解决这一问题，但成本高昂且不灵活。因此，研究动机是开发一种在不改变模型权重的情况下，通过进化智能体外壳组件来减少模型-环境失配的实用方法。

Method: StarHarness框架采用三个核心方法：(1)根据基线失败行为对任务进行分层，构建紧凑的进化池；(2)将提议者可见的搜索任务与提议者隐藏的选择任务分离；(3)保留held-out任务用于评估泛化能力。该框架进化的外壳组件包括提示、任务框架、工具接口、技能、MCP支持的提供者、子代理结构和代理循环配置。

Result: 在ITBench SRE、EnterpriseOps-Gym ITSM和AutomationBench Finance三个基准测试上，经过4-12次接受更改后，全基准性能相比默认外壳提升20-35个百分点。这些改进在被排除进化的任务上持续存在，且无需重新进化即可在GPT和Qwen模型家族间迁移。追踪分析表明改进源于接口修复、环境约定和操作知识，减少了假阳性诊断并缩短了轨迹。

Conclusion: StarHarness为工具丰富的企业任务提供了一种减少模型-环境失配的实用方法，证明了在不改变模型权重的情况下，通过进化智能体外壳可以显著提升性能，且具有良好的泛化和跨模型迁移能力，为企业AI应用提供了一种高效灵活的解决方案。

Abstract: We present StarHarness, a framework for evolving environment-specific agent harnesses while keeping model weights fixed. The evolved harness can include prompt and task framing, tool interfaces, skills, MCP-backed providers, subagent structure, and agent-loop configuration. StarHarness constructs a compact evolution pool by stratifying tasks according to baseline failure behavior, separates proposer-visible search tasks from proposer-hidden selection tasks, and reserves held-out tasks for evaluating generalization. Across ITBench SRE, EnterpriseOps-Gym ITSM, and AutomationBench Finance, harness evolution improves full-benchmark performance by 20-35 percentage points over the default harness after 4-12 accepted changes per environment. These gains persist on tasks excluded from evolution and transfer without re-evolution across GPT and Qwen model families. Trace analysis links the improvements to interface repairs, environment conventions, and operational knowledge that compresses search, with fewer false-positive diagnoses and shorter trajectories in several settings. StarHarness therefore offers a practical way to reduce persistent model-environment mismatch in tool-rich enterprise tasks.

</details>


### [215] [Strictly Causal Streaming Video Anomaly Detection with a Theoretically-Grounded State-Space Core](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24810)
*Yogesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种严格的因果流式视频异常检测器，具有固定大小状态和O(1)时间/内存复杂度，在边缘硬件上实现1300+FPS，但准确率仍落后于非因果基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于Mamba的视频异常检测方法仍依赖内部缓冲，缺乏时间记忆与检测延迟关系的理论分析，且仅在GPU而非目标边缘硬件上评估效率。

Method: 设计了严格的因果流式检测器，其核心是对角线性状态空间递归（带输入和状态依赖的衰减门），通过冻结视觉骨干上的自监督因果下一嵌入预测进行训练，无前瞻无缓冲。

Result: 推导了递归衰减谱与检测延迟及可捕获最短异常的闭式关系。在Apple M3 Pro上实测端到端延迟0.74-0.77ms/帧（>1300FPS）。在Ped2和Avenue上达到67.9%和70.2%帧级AUC，落后于非因果SSM基线。门控贡献依赖数据集大小。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在流式视频异常检测中建立了延迟理论边界，证明了事件边界门控（而非基础衰减）主导响应性，并在真实边缘硬件上验证了高吞吐量。下一步是缩小与非因果方法的准确率差距并扩展到更大基准测试。

Abstract: Recent work has applied Mamba style state space models (SSMs) to video anomaly detection, yet existing approaches still rely on buffering clips or windows internally, lack a theoretical account of how temporal memory relates to detection latency, and benchmark efficiency only through GPU throughput rather than the edge hardware these methods are intended to target. We introduce a strictly causal streaming anomaly detector whose fixed size state is updated in O(1) time and memory per incoming frame, with no lookahead and no clip buffering. Its temporal core is a diagonal linear state space recurrence with an input and state dependent decay gate, trained self supervised through causal next embedding prediction on a frozen visual backbone. We derive a closed form relationship between the recurrence decay spectrum and both detection delay and the shortest anomaly it can reliably capture, then validate empirically on UCSD Ped2 and CUHK Avenue. The settling delay bound predicted from the learned base decay (57 to 59 frames) sits far above the measured detection delay (1.6 and 18.4 frames), showing that the event boundary gate, not the base decay, governs responsiveness. We further report end to end latency and throughput measured directly on Apple M3 Pro hardware, 0.74 ms and 0.77 ms per frame (over 1300 FPS), rather than simulated GPU numbers. With an untuned initial configuration the method reaches 67.9 percent and 70.2 percent frame level AUC on Ped2 and Avenue, trailing prior non causal SSM baselines in accuracy. Ablations over decay rate, state size, and gating reveal that the gate contribution is dataset size dependent, hurting accuracy on the smaller Ped2 training set but helping on the larger Avenue one. Closing this accuracy gap and extending evaluation to a third, larger benchmark are immediate next steps.

</details>


### [216] [Constrained Entity Selection under Partial Knowledge for LLM-Based Knowledge Graph QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24824)
*Emanuel Kitzelmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的LLM知识图谱问答方法，通过符号约束验证LLM生成的候选答案，在保证召回率的同时提高了准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM知识图谱问答方法存在两个主要问题：基于完整语义解析的方法（如SPARQL）在面对复杂模式或不完整知识图谱时表现脆弱；基于LLM推理和答案生成的方法虽然更稳健但缺乏形式化保证。作者希望找到一种折中方案，既能提供符号化的保证，又能保持系统的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出了"部分知识下的约束实体选择(CES-PK)"问题框架。首先由LLM生成候选答案，然后使用从问题中推导出的轻量级符号约束进行验证。针对不完整知识图谱，采用了三值约束语义(满足/违反/未知)，在开放世界假设下避免错误拒绝有效答案。主要约束类型包括类型约束、关系约束和排除约束。

Result: 在Hetionet生物医学知识图谱上的实验表明：通过过滤无效候选答案提高了精确率；由于保留了约束未被明确违反的候选答案，召回率得到保持；满足的约束为剩余候选答案提供了额外的正面符号证据，用于候选答案排序。

Conclusion: 该方法成功地在LLM的灵活性和符号推理的可解释性之间找到了平衡点，为知识图谱问答提供了一个既稳健又有形式化支持的解决方案，特别适用于处理不完整的真实世界知识图谱。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used for knowledge graph question answering (KGQA), but can fail to correctly ground answers in the underlying graph. Current approaches to LLM-based KGQA either rely on full semantic parsing into executable queries such as SPARQL, which is brittle in practice due to complex schemas or incompleteness of real-world KGs, or on LLM-reasoning and answer generation over KGs, which can be more robust but lacks formal guarantees. In this work, we study a complementary setting in which \emph{candidate} answers are generated by an LLM-based system and subsequently verified using lightweight symbolic constraints derived from the question. We introduce \emph{Constrained Entity Selection under Partial Knowledge (CES-PK)}, a problem formulation that focuses on eliminating invalid answers and providing symbolic support for valid ones without requiring construction of executable logical forms. To account for incomplete KGs, we employ a three-valued constraint semantics (\emph{satisfied, violated, unknown}) that avoids incorrect rejections under open-world assumptions. To demonstrate the effects of our method, we instantiate this framework over the Hetionet biomedical knowledge graph and evaluate the impact of type, relation, and exclusion constraints. Experiments show that precision improves by filtering invalid candidates, while recall is preserved due to retaining candidates whose constraints are not explicitly violated. Satisfied constraints provide additional positive symbolic evidence to rank remaining candidates.

</details>


### [217] [A Dual-Dimensional LLM Framework for Automated Item Incidental Content Similarity Analysis in Large-Scale Assessments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24825)
*Jing Huang,Jihong Zhang,Hua-Hua Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了基于大语言模型的双维度自动题目相似性分析框架(AISA)，通过结构分解和语义相关性两个维度评估题目相似性，实验证明该方法比传统文本相似性指标更能有效识别构念无关冗余，并在计算机化自适应测试中提升了估计稳定性并减少了偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大规模评估的快速扩展和自动题目生成的广泛应用，偶然性内容冗余问题日益严重，即措辞或情境框架等构念无关要素在题目间出现非预期的重复。传统的相似性指标(如BLEU或余弦相似度)无法同时捕捉驱动感知冗余的细微结构和语义层面。

Method: 提出了一种基于大语言模型的双维度自动题目相似性分析框架(AISA)，通过结构分解和语义相关性两个维度来操作化相似性评估，并进行了心理测量验证。研究还将该框架应用于计算机化自适应测试(CAT)中进行评估。

Result: 心理测量验证表明，基于LLM的相似性指标与构念无关的局部依赖指标更加一致，产生的题目参数分组比传统文本测量更加连贯。模拟实验显示，在题目选择中纳入基于LLM的相似性约束可以提高估计稳定性、减少偏差，且效率损失极小，表现优于基于传统指标的约束方法。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，基于LLM的AISA框架具有支持可扩展题库管理、内容感知的测试组卷以及跨多种评估情境的体验敏感型自适应测试的潜力，为大规模评估中的内容冗余问题提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of large-scale assessments and the growing adoption of automatic item generation have intensified concerns about incidental content redundancy, where construct-irrelevant elements such as wording or contextual framing become unintentionally repetitive across items. Traditional similarity metrics like BLEU or cosine similarity, often fail to capture the nuanced structural and semantic layers that drive perceived redundancy simultaneously. This study proposes a dual-dimensional framework for Automated Item Similarity Analysis (AISA) powered by Large Language Models (LLMs), operationalizing similarity through Structured Decomposition and Semantic Relatedness. Psychometric validation indicates that LLM-derived metrics align more closely with indicators of construct-irrelevant local dependence and yield more coherent item parameter groupings than traditional text-based measures. The framework is further evaluated through its application in Computerized Adaptive Testing (CAT). Simulations reveal that incorporating LLM-based similarity constraints into item selection improves estimation stability and reduces bias with minimal efficiency trade-offs, outperforming constraints based on conventional metrics. These findings highlight the potential of LLM-powered AISA to support scalable bank curation, content-aware test assembly, and experience-sensitive adaptive testing across diverse assessment contexts.

</details>


### [218] [FedV-KGQA: Multi-Hop Question Answering over Vertically Partitioned Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24846)
*Md Saikat Islam Khan Bappy,Oshani Seneviratne*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FedV-KGQA框架，在垂直划分的知识图谱上实现多跳问答，通过本地图丰富和嵌入技术保护数据隐私，性能接近中心化系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的数据因治理和主权约束分布在不同组织中，形成垂直划分的知识孤岛。现有中心化系统无法回答跨孤岛的多跳问题，需要一种既能保护数据隐私又能进行跨组织多跳推理的解决方案。

Method: 1. 结合本地图丰富和知识图谱嵌入技术，确保原始三元组和关系参数不离开各数据孤岛；2. 引入主题实体锚定机制，在无需运行时跨孤岛通信的情况下将问题定位到正确的图邻域；3. 在结构数据边界内实现多跳推理，无需集中式图访问。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上评估了12种模型配置，FedV-KGQA表现强劲：性能接近中心化系统；能够泛化到3跳推理；对嵌入扰动具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FedV-KGQA成功解决了垂直划分知识图谱上的多跳问答问题，在保护数据隐私的同时实现了与中心化系统相当的性能，为跨组织知识推理提供了可行的隐私保护解决方案。

Abstract: Real-world data for knowledge graph question answering is often distributed across different organizations due to governance and data sovereignty constraints. While centralized systems exist, they cannot answer multi-hop questions when the required facts are split across vertically partitioned silos. In this paper, we propose FedV-KGQA, a framework for multi-hop reasoning over knowledge graphs in which organizations share entities but own disjoint sets of relations. Our approach combines local graph enrichment and knowledge graph embeddings to ensure raw triples and relation parameters never leave each silo, establishing a structural data boundary without requiring centralized graph access. We further introduce a topic entity anchoring mechanism that grounds questions in the correct graph neighborhood without any runtime inter-silo communication. We evaluate 12 model configurations across three benchmarks and show that FedV-KGQA performs strongly, remains close to centralized performance, generalizes to 3-hop reasoning, and is robust to embedding perturbations.

</details>


### [219] [SPO++: Stream-Aligned Policy Optimization for Asynchronous Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24870)
*Kai Ruan,Jinghao Lin,Qianshan Wei,Ziqi Zhou,Zihe Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SPO++通过修正轨迹中心化不匹配问题（使用动作token度量标准化）和按策略事件组织证据，显著提升了在线学习效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单流策略优化（SPO）中的轨迹中心化（trajectory centering）通常无法正确中心化actor实际消费的token加权量，导致优势估计与策略优化之间存在不匹配，影响学习效果。

Method: 提出SPO++方法：1) 在动作token度量（action-token measure）下标准化终局优势（terminal-outcome advantages）以修正中心化问题；2) 按生成证据的策略事件（policy event）而非学习者接收顺序来组织提示证据。

Result: 在ALFWorld（两种模型规模）和Math-TIR基准测试中，SPO++相比SPO在线学习效率显著提升；消融实验表明动作token度量归一化是最强的改进组件。

Conclusion: 正确处理动作token度量下的优势标准化对于策略优化至关重要，SPO++为工具使用轨迹等长序列的强化学习提供了更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Group-relative reinforcement learning waits for sibling rollouts of the same prompt, which is costly for long and variable tool-use trajectories. Single-stream Policy Optimization (SPO) removes this dependency with a persistent prompt-level value estimate, but its recipe whitens one advantage per trajectory before optimizing a token-mean actor loss. We show that trajectory centering generally does not center the token-weighted quantity consumed by the actor, and fix the mismatch by standardizing terminal-outcome advantages under the action-token measure. We additionally organize prompt evidence by the policy event that generated it rather than learner receipt order. Across matched runs on ALFWorld at two model scales and on Math-TIR, SPO++ improves online learning efficiency over SPO. A paired ablation identifies action-token-measure normalization as the strongest tested component.

</details>


### [220] [Recursive Experiential-Working Memory Evolution for Long-Horizon Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24876)
*Zhaochen Yu,Yingcheng Wu,Zhenfei Yin,Kaiyuan Chen,Zhe Zhao,Mengdi Wang,Shuicheng Yan,Ling Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Recuris，一种递归经验-工作记忆架构，通过工作记忆跟踪任务进度并指导技能选择，解决了长程任务中递归自我改进的难题。在四个长程基准测试和十个模型上，35/37个实验对中任务成功率显著提升，使前沿模型达到SOTA水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归自我改进(RSI)在长程任务中面临两大挑战：随着交互历史增长，任务状态变得模糊，且技能调用容易出现错位。传统的全历史方法难以有效处理长程依赖和故障定位问题。

Method: 提出Recuris架构，包含工作记忆和经验记忆的耦合机制。工作记忆跟踪任务进度并从经验记忆中指导技能选择，将技能使用锚定于当前需求而非完整历史。执行过程转化为结构化证据，定位故障到特定记忆组件。固定元代理将证据转化为局部化、验证门控的技能记忆更新，形成有界的递归记忆演化循环。

Result: 在四个长程基准测试和十个模型上，37个实验对中35个获得任务成功率提升。在tau-bench上为GPT-5.6 Sol提升17.8分，Claude Opus 5提升15.6分(达到87.9%)；在SkillFlow上为Qwen3.6-27B/35B分别提升16.6/13.5分。最长任务上优势扩大至32.2分，常见长程失败减少高达80%。

Conclusion: 递归演化记忆为RSI提供了可扩展的基础，使智能体能够持续将累积经验转化为更有效的长程行为。该方法成功将前沿模型推向SOTA水平的任务成功率，证明了记忆架构创新对长程智能体的重要性。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) remains hard in long-horizon tasks, where growing histories obscure the task state and misalign skill invocation. We introduce Recuris, a recursive Experiential-Working Memory architecture for long-horizon agent harnesses, in which Working Memory tracks task progress and guides skill selection from Experiential Memory, grounding skill use in current needs rather than the full history. This coupling also turns execution into structured evidence that localizes failures to specific memory components. Across tasks, a fixed Meta-Agent turns that evidence into localized, validation-gated updates to Skill Memory that reshape execution and yield new evidence, forming a bounded recursive memory-evolution loop. Across four long-horizon benchmarks and ten models, Recuris improves task success in 35 of the 37 completed model-benchmark pairs, carrying frontier models to SOTA-level task success: on tau-bench it adds +17.8 points to GPT-5.6 Sol and +15.6 to Claude Opus 5, taking Opus 5 to 87.9%, and +16.6/+13.5 points on Qwen3.6-27B/35B on SkillFlow. The advantage widens as the interaction horizon grows, to +32.2 points on the longest tasks, and common long-horizon failures fall by up to 80%. These results position recursively evolving memory as a scalable foundation for RSI, enabling agents to continuously transform accumulated experience into increasingly effective long-horizon behavior. Code: https://github.com/Gen-Verse/Recuris

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [221] [Markets, Not Planners: Decentralized Orchestration of LLM Agents with Private Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23867)
*Xiao Liu,Haoyang Li,Songwei Li,Hongbo Fang,Fengli Xu,Feng Shi,James Evans*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了AgentLance，一个基于劳动力市场的去中心化LLM代理编排系统，通过竞价机制和VCG支付规则实现了比中心化编排更高效、更公平的任务分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM代理数量激增，现有的中心化编排机制面临三大问题：1）中心化规划器成为性能瓶颈；2）需要代理的私有信息（如执行成本）；3）易受操纵，单个偏好插入可使特定代理的任务份额近乎翻倍。现有机制无法适应异构代理池的高效编排需求。

Method: 提出AgentLance重复劳动力市场机制：1）代理使用私有成本和自维护策略笔记对任务竞价；2）分配器基于竞价和公开声誉记录选择获胜者；3）采用VCG风格支付规则奖励成本感知竞价；4）通过分层委托处理复杂任务，获胜代理可分解任务并通过相同机制转包。

Result: 在数学推理、代码生成、知识密集型QA和代理任务上的实验表明：1）AgentLance能将代理匹配到其专长领域；2）随着成本敏感性增加，工作会转移到更便宜的代理；3）持续优于单模型、中心化编排和市场基线方法。诊断并修正市场失灵（包括不准确成本自估计和次优竞价）可获得进一步改进。

Conclusion: 去中心化的劳动力市场机制为LLM代理编排提供了更高效、更抗操纵的解决方案，为构建更高效的代理经济体系指明了方向。通过识别和纠正市场失灵可进一步提升系统性能。

Abstract: As LLM agents proliferate, built by different parties and with different capabilities and costs, orchestrating them is more like assembling labor across the economy than a computer calling a subroutine. Existing orchestration is typically centralized, with a single planner assigning every task, but this creates a bottleneck as agent pools grow, requires private information (e.g., agents' execution costs), and can easily be manipulated, such that a single inserted preference nearly doubles a favored agent's task share under a centralized LLM allocator. We introduce AgentLance, a repeated labor market in which agents bid on tasks using their private costs and self-maintained strategy notes, an allocator selects winners from bids and public reputation records, and a VCG-style payment rule rewards cost-aware bidding. Complex tasks are handled by hierarchical delegation: winning agents can decompose work and subcontract it through the same mechanism. Across mathematical reasoning, code generation, knowledge-intensive QA, and agentic tasks, AgentLance matches agents to their specializations, shifts work toward cheaper agents as cost sensitivity rises, and consistently outperforms single-model, centralized-orchestration, and market baselines. Diagnosing market failures, including inaccurate cost self-estimation and sub-optimal bidding, then correcting them in controlled experiments yields further gains, charting a path toward more efficient agent economies.

</details>


### [222] [Agentopia on a Consumer GPU: A Reduced-Scale Long-Horizon Port with an 8B Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24215)
*Luo Huan*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文在消费级硬件（单张RTX 5070 Ti，12GB显存）上实现了Agentopia的缩减规模移植，使用4-bit量化模型成功完成了约150周的模拟，验证了小规模部署的可行性，并提出了三项结构优化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始Agentopia使用100个智能体和大规模模型（Qwen3.5-397B-A17B）进行了10年模拟，但在消费级硬件上的缩减规模部署行为尚不明确。本研究旨在填补这一空白，验证在资源受限环境下运行多智能体社会模拟的可行性。

Method: 在单张NVIDIA RTX 5070 Ti（12GB显存）上使用Qwen3-8B-AWQ（4-bit量化模型）实现Agentopia移植。引入三项结构适配：(1)系统管理的分层记忆压缩，(2)每个模拟日四个活动块，(3)显式的生理和心理健康状态变量。进行了三次独立随机运行。

Result: 三次运行共完成154系统周（770智能体周），其中两次完成52周，一次完成50周后达到上下文限制。无智能体死亡，无阈值型健康警告被记录。包含至少一个NO_RESPONSE字段的活动记录发生率在10.15-10.29%之间。对比实验表明分层记忆影响产出，四时间块设计增加2.72倍最终化记录但缺失字段率更高。

Conclusion: 成功验证了在消费级硬件上运行缩减规模多智能体社会模拟的可行性。虽然对比实验未支持因果行为主张，但证明了通过结构适配和模型量化可在有限资源下实现稳定的长周期模拟，并公开了验证配置、审计数据和实现代码。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent social simulation has demonstrated compelling results, but Agentopia was evaluated with 100 agents over 10 simulated years using Qwen3.5-397B-A17B, leaving the behavior of reduced-scale deployments on consumer hardware unclear. In this paper, we implement and evaluate a reduced-scale Agentopia port on a single NVIDIA RTX 5070 Ti(12 GB VRAM) using Qwen3-8B-AWQ, a 4-bit quantized model. We introduce three structural adaptations for this setting: (1) system-managed layered memory compression, (2) four activity blocks per simulated day, and (3) explicit physical- and mental-health state variables. Across three independent stochastic runs, two runs completed 52 weeks and the third completed 50 weeks before reaching the context limit, totaling 154 system-weeks (770 agent-weeks). No agent died,and no threshold-based health warning was logged; activity records containing at least one NO_RESPONSE field occurred at rates of 10.15-10.29% across runs. A 52-week memory-off run tied L2/L3 artifact production to layered memory; a separate 10-week comparison associated four daily time blocks with 2.72 times more finalized records and lower lexical duplication, but a higher missing-field rate. These comparisons do not support causal behavioral claims. We release validated configurations, derived audits, analysis scripts, aggregate figure data, and our implementation changes in a public fork; raw runs and initial persona data are excluded because their redistribution provenance is not fully resolved.

</details>


### [223] [Test-Time Collaborative Classification over Multi-Agent Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24787)
*Ping Hu,Mert Kayaalp,Ali H. Sayed*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 针对多智能体系统异构性挑战，提出了一个分布式二分类协作框架，采用独立训练与协同推理模式，通过交换局部决策统计实现测试时智能体间的协调预测，并提供了分类错误保证和泛化界理论分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统日益增长的异构性（架构、特征空间或模态差异）对联合训练全局模型带来显著挑战；同时智能体间的协同推理已被公认为网络分布式决策的强大机制。这些观察促使作者探索独立训练与协同推理相结合的新范式。

Method: 提出分布式二分类协作框架：(1) 独立训练阶段——各智能体独立训练，允许架构、特征空间或模态差异；(2) 协同推理阶段——测试时通过分布式学习协议交换局部决策统计，形成集体预测。研究涵盖不同通信预算和分布式学习规则下的性能表现。

Result: 建立了充分通信、有限轮次通信和有限精度通信条件下的分类错误保证；推导了PAC式泛化界；理论结果捕捉了模型异构性、网络拓扑、组合策略和通信约束对预测精度的影响。实验与理论结果共同揭示了独立训练的代价和集体预测的收益。

Conclusion: 独立训练与协同推理范式为异构多智能体系统提供了一种有效的分布式决策方案。研究量化了模型异构性、网络结构和通信约束对系统性能的影响，证明了集体预测相比独立预测的性能增益，为实际应用提供了理论指导和性能保障。

Abstract: The increasing heterogeneity of multi-agent systems poses significant challenges for jointly training a global model across agents. At the same time, cooperative inference between agents has long been recognized as a powerful mechanism for distributed decision making over networks. Motivated by these observations, we propose a collaboration framework for distributed binary classification over multi-agent networks, where a set of independently trained agents, potentially differing in architecture, feature space, or modality, coordinate their actions during test time to form collective predictions. This coordination is achieved by exchanging local decision statistics through a distributed learning protocol. We develop a theoretical and experimental study of this independent training and cooperative inference paradigm, and examine its performance under different communication budgets and distributed learning rules. We establish classification error guarantees under sufficient, finite-round, and finite-precision communication, together with PAC-style generalization bounds. These results capture the influence of model heterogeneity, network topology, combination policy, and communication constraints on prediction accuracy. Taken together with the experimental results, they reveal both the price of independent training and the benefit of collective prediction for the proposed distributed decision making framework with models learned from data.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [224] [Transformer Accelerator (TFA): A Macro-Op INT8 Hardware Chip for Transformer Inference and Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23582)
*Shashank*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了TFA，一个可综合、可参数化的INT8内存到内存的Transformer推理加速器，通过单一时分复用数据通路处理提示和自回归生成，在SkyWater sky130工艺上实现了2.73 mm²的逻辑面积，验证配置相比22线程CPU实现了约20倍的端到端加速，更大设计预计可将每个token的能耗降低约1000倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着Transformer模型在自然语言处理等领域的广泛应用，其推理计算面临高延迟和高能耗的挑战。现有的通用处理器难以高效处理Transformer中的矩阵乘法、softmax、归一化等多样化运算，缺乏针对INT8量化推理的专用硬件支持。因此，开发一个紧凑、高效且可综合的硬件加速器，以实现预训练Transformer模型的端到端、位精确执行成为迫切需求。

Method: TFA采用单一时分复用数据通路处理提示处理和自回归生成两个阶段，通过八个512位宏操作描述符实现矩阵乘法、softmax、RMSNorm、元素级运算和复制/聚集操作。RTL设计结合了输出固定型累加乘数组与乒乓缓冲器以重叠DMA和计算，实现了位精确的倒数平方根和除法单元、键值缓存和嵌入寻址以及安全中止的零填充写入引擎。采用UVM验证环境进行字节级比较，编译了t5-small编码器-解码器模型进行多语言翻译测试，并应用随机Hadamard重参数化提升INT8信号噪声比。

Result: 在25个测试和34个约束随机运行中，TFA实现了零失配、100%功能覆盖率和94.96%代码覆盖率。执行了70,320个描述符并与37.9 MB的黄金模型输出完美匹配。INT8输出在五个句子上与浮点参考实现token对token完全匹配，其余产生有效的替代翻译。随机Hadamard重参数化在各层恢复约11 dB的INT8信噪比。验证配置相比22线程CPU实现约20倍加速，逻辑面积在SkyWater sky130工艺下为2.73 mm²，更大设计预计能耗降低约1000倍。

Conclusion: TFA成功展示了使用紧凑硬件和编译器管理的量化技术实现预训练Transformer模型的端到端、位精确执行。该工作证明了专用加速器能够在保持输出质量的同时显著提升推理效率和降低能耗，为Transformer模型的边缘部署和高效推理提供了可行的硬件解决方案。

Abstract: We present the Transformer Accelerator (TFA), a synthesizable, parameterizable INT8 memory-to-memory engine for transformer inference. One time-multiplexed datapath handles prompt processing and autoregressive generation. TFA implements matrix multiplication, softmax, RMSNorm, elementwise, and copy/gather operations through eight 512-bit macro-op descriptors. Offline-compiled programs are fetched, validated, and dispatched through AXI interfaces, supporting encoder, decoder, and encoder-decoder models.
  The RTL combines an output-stationary multiply-accumulate array with ping-pong buffers that overlap DMA and compute, bit-exact reciprocal-square-root and divide units, key-value-cache and embedding addressing, and an abort-safe zero-padding write engine. A UVM environment byte-compares outputs against a bit-exact golden model. Across 25 tests and 34 constrained-random runs, TFA achieved zero mismatches, 100% functional coverage, and 94.96% code coverage.
  We compiled the t5-small encoder-decoder pipeline for English-to-French, German, and Romanian translation. On ten multilingual proverbs, TFA executed 70,320 descriptors and matched 37.9 MB of golden-model output with zero mismatches. INT8 output matched the floating-point reference token-for-token on five sentences; the rest produced valid alternative translations. Randomized-Hadamard reparameterization recovered about 11 dB of per-tensor INT8 signal-to-noise ratio across layers. The verification configuration achieved about 20x end-to-end speedup over a 22-thread CPU, while larger designs are projected to reduce energy per token by about 1000x. After RAM inference recoding, logic area fell to 2.73 mm2, and the design completed design-rule-clean synthesis and place-and-route on SkyWater sky130. TFA demonstrates end-to-end, bit-exact execution of pretrained transformers using compact hardware and compiler-managed quantization.

</details>


### [225] [Squeezing the Cache, Preserving the Truth: Monotonic Equipotential Allocation with Geodesia-KV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23599)
*Vincenzo Dentamaro,Pancrazio Auteri,Giuseppe Pirlo*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出Geodesia-KV，一种无需训练的KV缓存压缩策略家族，通过单调块级精度分配、精确率失真残差和查询稀疏读取实现硬件友好的压缩，在16 GiB GPU上实现百万token上下文生成，峰值显存节省高达71.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存压缩性能评估存在两个关键问题：混淆常驻比特与读取带宽、以及块状教师强制伪影的影响，导致无法准确评估真实的硬件可用压缩性能。

Method: 提出Geodesia-KV方法，包含三个核心组件：（1）单调块级精度分配策略；（2）精确率失真残差计算；（3）查询稀疏读取机制。方法实现了常驻比特与读取比特的正确分离，并作为vLLM的原生GeodesiaKVCacheManager插件实现，通过单调比特降级完全消除密集缓存常驻需求。

Result: 在WikiText-2 16k上下文任务中，Geodesia-KV的5比特操作点在Qwen模型上以更低比特率实现更低困惑度（优于KIVI-4）。在PG-19上，压缩Quest版本在困惑度、常驻率（9.83 vs 16.25比特/值）和读取率（1.95 vs 2.32比特/值）上均优于基线稀疏方法。在16 GiB GPU上实现百万token上下文生成，峰值显存节省高达71.7%。

Conclusion: Geodesia-KV通过正确的常驻与读取比特分离及因果评估，显著优于其他方法，在主流架构（Qwen、Llama、DeepSeek）上实现了高效的硬件级KV缓存压缩，使消费级硬件能够支持超长上下文生成。

Abstract: Current assessment of KV-cache compression performance confuses resident bits with read bandwidth and is affected by the artifacts of chunked teacher-forcing. We present Geodesia-KV, a family of training-free KV cache policies based on monotonic block-wise precision allocation, exact rate-distortion residuals, and query-sparse reading, enabling proper hardware-ready compression. With proper separation of resident and read bits and causal evaluation, we show that Geodesia-KV significantly outperforms other approaches. Specifically, on WikiText-2 with 16k context, the 5-bit operating point of Geodesia-KV results in lower perplexity at lower bitrate than KIVI-4 on Qwen. In addition, our compressed-Quest version delivers improved perplexity and reduces resident (9.83 vs 16.25 bits/value) and read rates (1.95 vs 2.32 bits/value) over baseline sparse methods on PG-19. As Geodesia-KV is implemented as native GeodesiaKVCacheManager plug-in of vLLM, Geodesia-KV fully removes the need for dense cache residency via monotonic bit demotion. With the full consumer hardware evaluation, Geodesia-KV leads to 1M-token context generation on a single 16 GiB GPU with up to 71.7% peak VRAM savings on all leading architectures (Qwen, Llama, DeepSeek).

</details>


### [226] [StateTune: Transforming LLM-Assisted EDA Flow Tuning into a Stateful, Closed-Loop Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23601)
*Kunlong Li,Shangshang Yao,Su Zheng,Lingli Wang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出StateTune框架，通过引入持久优化记忆将LLM辅助的EDA参数调优转变为闭环状态携带过程，在工业流程中实现所有测试基准的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EDA流程参数调优对结果质量至关重要，但参数空间巨大、参数间强耦合，且完整评估成本极高。现有基于LLM的调优工具仅将LLM作为外部提议器，使用瞬时工作上下文，缺乏状态记忆机制。

Method: StateTune将LLM辅助EDA调优重构为闭环状态携带过程。核心是类型化、证据门控的持久优化记忆，该记忆在每次评估后更新并在候选生成与预算分配间共享。在此基础上，采用期望超体积改进(EHVI)引导的运行时感知提升策略，按单位运行时间成本的期望帕累托前沿增益对候选方案排序。

Result: 在Cadence工业流程上评估（6个基准块，2个技术节点×3个设计），对比5个基线（包括LLM+RAG和偏好贝叶斯优化），StateTune在全部6个基准块上获得最强最终超体积，在最坏负松弛、面积和功耗上匹配或超越最强基线。消融实验显示持久记忆贡献最大：移除后超体积下降58.5%。

Conclusion: 持久优化记忆设计有效提升了EDA参数调优性能，证据门控敏感性、记忆污染、跨设计迁移及三种子可重复性分析（CV<7%）进一步验证了该设计。状态携带的闭环方法相比传统外部提议器方式具有显著优势。

Abstract: EDA flow parameter tuning is critical for quality-of-results~(QoR), yet the parameter space is large, tightly coupled, and full evaluations are prohibitively expensive. Prior LLM-assisted tuners mainly use the LLM as an external proposer with transient working context; we instead present \textbf{StateTune}, which reformulates LLM-assisted EDA tuning as a closed-loop, state-carrying process. Its optimizer state is a typed, evidence-gated \emph{persistent optimization memory} that is updated by every evaluation and shared between candidate generation and budget allocation. On top of this optimizer state, an expected hypervolume improvement (EHVI)-guided, runtime-aware promotion policy ranks quick-stage candidates by expected Pareto frontier gain per unit of runtime cost. Evaluated on a Cadence industrial flow across six benchmark blocks (two technology nodes \(\times\) three designs), against five baselines including LLM+retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) and preference-based Bayesian optimization (BO) tuners, StateTune achieves the strongest final hypervolume on all six benchmark blocks, showing a stable improvement in frontier quality across the full matrix; it also matches or surpasses the strongest baselines on worst negative slack (WNS), area, and power across the same set. Ablation shows persistent memory is the largest contributor: removing it costs 58.5\% of the hypervolume. Dedicated analyses of evidence-gating sensitivity, memory poisoning, cross-design transfer, and three-seed reproducibility (CV\,\(<\)\,7\% on five of six blocks) further validate the memory design.

</details>


### [227] [PACT: Post-route Agentic Checkpoint Tuning for FPGA Timing Closure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23602)
*Huan Lin,Kunlong Li,Lingli Wang,Zhiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出PACT框架，通过智能体驱动的后布线检查点调优，在保证设计合法性前提下，将FPGA设计的最大工作频率平均提升22.30%，相比现有方法速度提升6.4倍且成本降低24.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FPGA后布线阶段的时序收敛是一个关键挑战。现有的工程变更指令(ECO)优化方法在自动化方面仍存在困难：后布线修改必须在满足路由合法性、保持时序和脉冲宽度约束、检查点可重放性以及功能等价性的前提下改善时序，这需要处理复杂的约束和验证问题。

Method: 提出PACT（后布线智能体检查点调优）框架，将后布线调优建模为验证门控的检查点状态转换。框架包含：(1)基于检查点证据的智能体决策系统；(2)面向类型化Vivado和RapidWright技能的性能驱动配方规划器；(3)隔离工作空间中的工具行为探测机制；(4)证据门控案例记录系统，用于指导候选生成并抑制不安全、不支持或无效的操作。

Result: 在35个UltraScale+后布线检查点上的实验表明：PACT在验证通过的设计上将最大工作频率(Fmax)平均提升22.30%，优于DATuner(15.14%)和Codex Agent(9.78%)；相比DATuner速度提升6.4倍；每个DCP的平均令牌成本仅0.16美元，比自由形式的Codex Agent低24.5倍。

Conclusion: PACT成功实现了高效、低成本且自动化的FPGA后布线时序优化，通过验证门控的状态转换机制和智能体驱动的决策系统，在保证设计合法性的前提下显著提升了设计性能，为FPGA后布线优化提供了一种有效的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Late-stage FPGA timing closure often starts from an implemented design whose remaining violations are visible in timing reports. Engineering change order (ECO) optimization is a standard mechanism for applying localized changes to such designs without restarting the full implementation flow. Automating post-route ECO optimization remains challenging. A post-route change must improve timing without violating routing legality, hold or pulse-width timing constraints, checkpoint replayability or functional equivalence. This paper presents PACT, a Post-route Agentic Checkpoint Tuning framework for Vivado design checkpoints (DCP). PACT represents post-route tuning as validation-gated transitions between accepted and candidate checkpoints. From checkpoint-derived evidence, an agent selects localized backend actions through a profile-driven recipe planner over typed Vivado and RapidWright skills, and probes tool behavior in isolated workspaces. PACT records each trial as an evidence-gated case to guide candidate generation and suppress unsafe, unsupported or ineffective actions. Across 35 UltraScale+ post-route checkpoints, PACT improves validation-clean $F_{\max}$ (maximum operating frequency) by a geometric mean of $+22.30\%$ over the original implementations, compared with $+15.14\%$ for DATuner and $+9.78\%$ for the Codex Agent. On shared designs, PACT achieves these gains $6.4\times$ faster than the uncapped DATuner and at an average token cost of only \$0.16 per DCP ($24.5\times$ lower than the free-form Codex Agent). The source code is available in an anonymous repository

</details>


### [228] [Elastic KV Cache for LLM Serving:A Working Reclamation Mechanism, and Why Chunked Prefill Already Closes the Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23658)
*Sathishkumar Sivashanmugam*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种弹性KV缓存机制，试图回收LLM推理引擎中为最坏情况预填充预留的内存，但实验表明该机制收益有限，通过简单降低批处理令牌数参数能获得更好的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM服务引擎在启动时会为最坏情况的预填充激活预留内存空间，但在解码主导阶段该预留空间处于闲置状态，且无法分配给KV池使用。作者希望探索是否可以回收这部分预留内存以提高内存利用率。

Method: 提出了一种弹性KV缓存机制，在解码阶段将预留内存借给KV池，在预填充前归还。该机制基于CUDA虚拟内存路径实现，每个层使用两个物理句柄映射到一个连续虚拟范围，无需修改注意力内核或驱动程序。支持CUDA图和前缀缓存，解提交耗时几毫秒，重新提交耗时几十毫秒。

Result: 实验得到了一个诚实的负面结果：该机制仅在小的预填充块大小严重影响预填充延迟时才有收益。在受控实验中，不同块大小（8192与32768 tokens）的时间到首令牌差异仅约1%，因为预填充是计算受限且解码每步仅消耗约一个令牌。简单降低max_num_batched_tokens参数比该控制器能回收更多KV空间且延迟相近。在张量并行下，预留空间占比从TP1的16%降至TP4的2.7%。

Conclusion: 预留内存回收机制已实现并发布为可复用的用户空间弹性VMM分配器，但实验表明其收益有限。作者明确指出了在何种情况下回收预留内存仍然有帮助，为后续研究提供了参考。

Abstract: An LLM serving engine sizes its key-value (KV) cache once, at startup, permanently setting aside a reserve for the worst-case prefill activation. During decode-dominant phases that reserve sits idle, yet it cannot be handed to the KV pool because it is exactly the memory a large prefill needs. We ask whether this reserve is reclaimable, and build a mechanism to test it. Our elastic KV cache lends the reserve to the KV pool during decode and returns it before prefill, driven by the scheduler's one-step-ahead view of the next batch. It is pure userspace on the CUDA virtual-memory path: two physical handles mapped into one contiguous virtual range per layer, so the attention kernel is unchanged and no driver patch is required. It decommits in a few milliseconds and recommits in tens of milliseconds, works with CUDA graphs and prefix caching, and never triggers an out-of-memory event. A static commit of the same memory is unsafe, crashing on prefill bursts, which makes the dynamic toggle necessary.
  Having built the mechanism, we test the premise it rests on and report an honest negative result. It only pays off if a small prefill chunk size badly hurts prefill latency. In a controlled experiment injecting long prompts into a live decode load, that penalty is small (median time-to-first-token differs by about 1% between chunk sizes of 8192 and 32768 tokens), because prefill is compute bound and decode consumes only about one token per sequence per step. Simply lowering max_num_batched_tokens recovers more KV than the controller does, at nearly equal latency. The reserve also dilutes under tensor parallelism, from 16% of KV at TP1 to 2.7% at TP4. We state precisely when reclaiming the reserve could still help, and release the mechanism as a reusable userspace elastic-VMM allocator.

</details>


### [229] [Pipeline-Native Transformers: Co-Designing Model Architecture and CPU Inference for Bandwidth-Efficient Autoregressive Decode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23841)
*Tom Poperszky*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 针对CPU上单token自回归解码受限于内存带宽的问题，提出了CPU优先的流式推理引擎cflow和流水线原生Transformer架构，通过协同设计模型架构和推理运行时，在30.9B参数MoE模型上实现了5.94 tok/s的解码速度，显著优于现有CPU推理框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代CPU上单token自回归解码受内存带宽限制（约50 GB/s）而非计算能力（约1 TFLOP/s），每个生成的token都需要流式传输所有活跃权重一次，导致推理效率低下。

Method: 提出cflow CPU优先流式引擎与流水线原生Transformer架构协同设计：将权重按L2缓存大小的分块存储于计算消费顺序中，仅读取MoE层的top-k专家，融合投影操作，执行延迟感知的调度策略；架构采用垂直阶段优先的执行调度，优化层间依赖图。

Result: 在TinyStories训练的架构中，arch2_4_combined在困惑度相差仅0.24的情况下，将关键路径权重带宽减少2.00倍（9.00→4.50 MB/token），分块布局比行主序基线减少7.29倍L1数据读取缺失；在32核Ice Lake服务器上，30.9B参数MoE模型解码速度达5.94 tok/s，超过llama.cpp（4.75）和vLLM CPU后端（1.65）；磁盘驻留专家层的异步I/O重叠实现最高1.68倍加速。

Conclusion: 通过模型架构与推理运行时的协同设计可以有效缓解CPU推理的内存带宽瓶颈；实验验证了部分设计声明，并有1项被反驳、1项结论不明，完整报告了各项声明成立的条件。

Abstract: Single-token autoregressive decode on CPUs is bound by memory bandwidth, not arithmetic: a modern CPU sustains roughly 1 TFLOP/s of compute but only about 50 GB/s from main memory, and each generated token must stream every active weight once. This report argues that the most effective response is to co-design the model architecture and the inference runtime together. It presents cflow, a CPU-first streaming engine, alongside a family of pipeline-native transformer architectures whose inter-layer dependency graphs are constructed to permit a vertical, stage-major execution schedule.
  cflow stores weights as L2-sized tiles in compute-consumption order, reads only the top-k experts of each mixture-of-experts layer, fuses projections, and executes a delay-aware schedule from per-model dependency parameters. Across five architectures trained on TinyStories, one (arch2_4_combined) achieves a 2.00x reduction in critical-path weight bandwidth (9.00 to 4.50 MB/token) within 0.24 perplexity of the best candidate, and the tile layout incurs 7.29x fewer L1-data read misses than a row-major baseline. On a 30.9-billion-parameter pipeline-native MoE, cflow decodes at 5.94 tokens/s (tok/s) on a 32-vCPU Ice Lake server, ahead of llama.cpp (4.75) and the vLLM CPU backend (1.65) on comparably sized dense models. Realizing the expert-delay window as asynchronous I/O overlap on a disk-resident expert tier yields a further net win of up to 1.68x, matching the overlap model within 1%. Measurement refutes one of the eight design claims and leaves a second inconclusive; both are reported in full, with the conditions under which they would hold.

</details>


### [230] [Thermal Tuning Overhead in Wafer-Scale Optical Interconnects for LLM MoE Training: A Cross-Layer Analysis and Ferroelectric-Based Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24637)
*Seongwon Yoon,Pin-Jun Chen,Shimeng Yu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文分析了晶圆级光互连在MoE模型中的热致调谐问题，并提出电光调谐方案，实现了2.7x-3.8x的加速比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型(特别是MoE架构)的快速扩展加剧了互连需求，专家并行执行具有通信密集特性。晶圆级光互连(DWDM)提供了更高带宽的可能路径，但传统基于微环谐振器(MRR)的链路依赖热光调谐，容易受到工作负载引起的热波动影响。

Method: 采用跨层分析方法，结合工作负载分析、数据包级网络仿真和瞬态热分析。在ht-sim仿真器中实现晶圆级拓扑，构建3D集成GPU/EIC/PIC堆栈的Ansys热模型。引入基于铁电材料的电光调谐机制替代热光调谐。

Result: 瞬态温度变化超过传统热光控制回路的跟踪能力，导致通信阶段的重复调谐停顿。消除调谐停顿后，在三个MoE模型上实现显著加速：Mixtral 8x7B加速2.7x，Qwen-MoE 14.3B加速3.8x，LLaMA-MoE 6.7B加速3.3x。

Conclusion: 最小化光子调谐延迟对于实现光互连在大规模AI系统中的性能潜力至关重要，电光调谐机制可有效解决热光调谐的性能瓶颈。

Abstract: The rapid scaling of large language models (LLMs), particularly mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures, has intensified interconnect demands because expert-parallel execution is communication-intensive. Wafer-scale optical interconnects based on dense wavelength-division multiplexing (DWDM) offer a promising path to higher bandwidth; however, conventional microring-resonator (MRR)-based links rely on thermo-optic tuning and are therefore vulnerable to workload-induced thermal fluctuations. In this work, we present a cross-layer analysis of wafer-scale optical interconnects for MoE workloads that combines workload profiling, packet-level network simulation, and transient thermal analysis. We implement a wafer-scale topology in the ht-sim simulator and construct an Ansys thermal model of a 3D-integrated GPU/EIC/PIC stack. Our results show that transient temperature variations can exceed the tracking capability of conventional thermo-optic control loops and thereby introduce repeated tuning stalls during communication phases. The stall durations injected into the network simulation are derived directly from the thermal model rather than assumed. We further evaluate a ferroelectric-based electro-optic tuning mechanism that removes the continuous thermal-tuning requirement. In a four-layer proxy simulation across three MoE models, eliminating the tuning stalls yields speedups of 2.7x for Mixtral 8x7B, 3.8x for Qwen-MoE 14.3B, and 3.3x for LLaMA-MoE 6.7B relative to the thermo-optic case. These results indicate that minimizing photonic tuning latency is important for realizing the performance potential of optical interconnects in large-scale AI systems.

</details>


### [231] [Simthesizer: An Agent-Driven Simulation Framework for LLM Serving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24650)
*Wonung Kim,Hyunmin Choi,Minsu Kim,Jaehong Cho,Yeongwook Kim,Jongse Park*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Borg是一个基于智能体驱动的模拟器开发框架，通过可组合的基础设施和合成器智能体，自动将自然语言需求转化为模拟器功能，解决了大语言模型服务系统模拟器开发滞后于实际系统演进的问题，实现了高保真度和高效率的模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大语言模型服务系统的设计空间快速扩展，但现有的模拟器采用单体式流水线架构，无法适应新兴的工作负载（如智能体工作流）和机制（如解耦服务），每次新增机制都需要侵入式重写，导致模拟器开发严重滞后于实际部署系统的演进，形成了日益扩大的开发鸿沟。

Method: Borg框架引入了可组合的模拟器基础设施，将完整的服务工作流（包括协调控制决策）统一表达为动态图；核心组件包括：（1）Borg模拟器——将服务流程实现为统一的动态图；（2）合成器智能体——一个受约束的编程智能体，在特定护栏和保真度验证机制下，将自然语言功能需求转化为模拟器扩展；（3）统一抽象层——确保扩展遵循vLLM实际系统的行为规范。该方法实现了一次模拟器构建支持多种功能的演进，而非为每个功能单独构建新模拟器。

Result: 实验结果表明：（1）在保真度方面，基于Borg构建的扩展相对于vLLM实际系统的平均吞吐量误差为2.51%，显著优于现有模拟器的6.03%；（2）在性能方面，在相同工作负载下，Borg的模拟速度比两个最先进的模拟器LLMServingSim2.0和Vidur分别快284.96倍和23.19倍。

Conclusion: Borg框架成功实现了智能体驱动的模拟器开发范式转变，通过可组合基础设施和自动化编程智能体的协同工作，有效弥合了快速演进的大语言模型服务系统与模拟器之间的开发鸿沟，为探索不断扩展的设计空间提供了高效、高保真的模拟工具。

Abstract: System-level simulation is an essential tool for exploring the rapidly expanding design space of LLM serving systems, where real deployments remain costly and often infeasible. However, modern LLM serving now evolves faster than human-driven simulator development can track, and emerging workloads and mechanisms, from agentic workflows to disaggregated serving, no longer fit the monolithic simulation pipeline that existing simulators assume. Each new mechanism therefore demands an invasive rewrite, leaving a widening development gap between deployed serving systems and the simulators that model them.
  To close this gap, we present Borg, a framework that realizes agent-driven simulator development. Borg introduces a composable simulator infrastructure that uniformly expresses the complete serving workflow, including the control decisions that coordinate it, and realizes it as a unified dynamic graph in Borg simulator. Synthesizer agent, a harnessed coding agent, then lowers natural-language feature requests onto this abstraction under simulator-specific guardrails and fidelity validation, evolving one shared simulator instead of building a new one for every feature. Under the same coding agent and harnesses, extensions built on Borg follow a vLLM-based real system with 2.51% average throughput error, versus 6.03% for extensions built on existing simulators. On identical workloads, Borg also simulates up to 284.96x and 23.19x faster than two state-of-the-art simulators, LLMServingSim2.0 and Vidur, respectively.

</details>


### [232] [Maia 200: A Software Defined Dataflow System for Large-scale AI Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24664)
*Sherry Xu,Marco Heddes,Jackson Peng,Tom Savell,Monica Tang,Prashant Ranjan,Jesse Benson,Ofer Dekel,Saurabh Dighe,Anupama Kurpad,Artour Levin,Matthew Mattina,George Petre,Cheng Tang,Yuan Yu,Li Zhang,Torsten Hoefler*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Maia 200，一种新型AI加速器，采用软件定义的本地访问数据流架构（SDLA），在750W TDP下实现10145 Tflop/s FP4和5072 Tflop/s FP8的高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI计算面临效率与可扩展性挑战，传统以线程为中心的架构已难以满足需求。作者提出需要转向以数据移动为中心的架构范式，以应对日益增长的AI推理工作负载对高性能计算的需求。

Method: 提出软件定义的本地访问数据流架构（SDLA）新架构类别，通过显式编程数据流引擎来协调高度专业化的内存和数据移动引擎。建立受Flynn分类启发的数据管理分类法，从线程中心转向数据移动中心的架构设计。

Result: Maia 200在750W TDP和7 TB/s HBM带宽条件下，达到10145 Tflop/s FP4和5072 Tflop/s FP8的性能表现，实现了显著的成本和能耗节约，同时支持AI推理工作负载的大规模并行。

Conclusion: SDLA架构通过以数据移动为中心的设计理念，有效解决了现代AI计算的效率与可扩展性问题，Maia 200为下一代高性能计算系统提供了极具竞争力的解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce Maia 200, an advanced AI accelerator delivering high performance-10 145 Tflop/s FP4 and 5072 Tflop/s FP8 within a 750W TDP and 7 TB/s HBM bandwidth. Maia exemplifies a new class of Software Defined Locally Accessed Dataflow Architectures (SDLA), which explicitly program dataflow engines to orchestrate highly specialized memories and data movement engines. This approach shifts the focus from today's thread-centric to data-movement-centric architecture, improving efficiency and scalability. Our taxonomy of data management, inspired by Flynn's classification, highlights how SDLA addresses challenges in modern AI computing. Maia 200 achieves significant cost and energy savings while supporting massive parallelism for AI inference workloads, making it a compelling solution for next-generation high-performance computing systems.

</details>
